(2) The 6-element `STAGE_ORDER` is hardcoded — the plan FORBIDS importing server `stage.ts` into the IIFE; if the server ladder ever changes, hand-sync this const. (3) Neglect uses a **strict `<` timestamp cutoff** (`Date.parse(lastOutbound) < now − 7d`) mirroring `followups.ts` — exactly-7d is NOT neglected, 8d is (the boundary test locks this). (4) The null-outbound neglect branch requires `lastInboundAt` present (a dropped inbound) — a never-touched target with no inbound is deliberately NOT neglected, else the whole roster lights up. **Freshness `NEGLECT_DAYS=7` is an opening guess** (= `NEGLECTED_TARGET_DAYS`), revisit with the Targets amber. Extension-only, 13 bun tests; gate with `bun run test` (**988/0**), not bare `bun test` (inspect.test.ts file-vs-`:memory:` trap unchanged — RU.3 gotcha). **Binds AX.3** (timeline revamp — xhigh, opens the D9 content.ts chain) and is parallel-independent of AX.4 (tweetContext view-model).
- **AX.4: `extension/src/shared/tweetContext.ts` is PURE and INERT** — `buildTweetContextModel(dossier, tweetId, nowMs)` + the `Dossier`/`TweetContextModel` types exist but NO consumer imports them yet (**AX.5** does the status-page DOM plumbing: one dossier fetch per handle, 5-min module cache, renders this model into `div.stratus-context-panel`, collapse flag `chrome.storage.local['augment:contextCollapsed']`). **The model contract AX.5 renders against:** `{header:{handle,displayName,stage,sinceDays,followersCount,momentumPerDay,tags[]}, relationship:{inbound,outbound,lastInboundAt,lastOutboundAt}, alreadyReplied:{postedTweetId,ageMin}|null, openLoops:{tweetId,text,ageDays}[], outcomes:{text,views,profileVisits,angle,postedAt}[], anglePreference:{angle,measured}|null, notes}`. **The module consumes the C1 dossier as HTTP JSON — every timestamp is an ISO string, never a Date** (the fixture test literal encodes this; AX.5 passes `Date.now()` as `nowMs`, all ages derive from it). **Design choices (D63, worth keeping):** (1) relationship COUNTS come from `events` (their_mention+their_reply_to_me vs my_reply), last DATES from the `people` watermark columns; (2) `outcomes[].angle` is null in production today — the dossier route does NOT emit per-outcome angle (only the aggregate `angles` crosstab), so it's typed optional + read `?? null`; if AX.5 wants it on the panel, add it to the dossier `outcomes` map, no view-model change needed; (3) `anglePreference` re-implements `pickAnglePreference` inline (gate ≥3 = `MIN_MEASURED_FOR_ANGLE_PREFERENCE`, best median profileVisits, views tie-break) to keep the IIFE free of server imports — hand-sync if the server pick ever changes; (4) `momentumPerDay` mirrors `authorMomentum`'s `Math.max(days,1)` clamp so same-day snapshots don't read as explosive, null <2 points. Extension-only, 15 bun tests; gate with `bun run test` (**1003/0**), not bare `bun test` (inspect.test.ts file-vs-`:memory:` trap unchanged — RU.3 gotcha). **Binds AX.5** (status-page context panel — xhigh, deps AX.3+AX.4, part of the D9 content.ts chain).
- **AX.5: the tweet-page context panel is LIVE in `content.ts`** (`syncContextPanel`/`renderContextPanel`, once/scan beside `syncAuthorButton`). `dossierCache` is a `Map<handle,{state,dossier,at}>` — **all 3 terminal states are cached** (`ready`/`missing`/`unavailable`) precisely so a status page never fetch-loops through the `.finally`→`scheduleScan()` re-render (the ONE deviation from the timeline glance cache, which relies on constant mutations). The panel goes **as the focused article's next sibling** (`insertAdjacentElement('afterend')` — parent flow, survives article-node recycling), keyed `data-renderKey`=`state:tweetId:handle:cachedAt` + a `nextElementSibling`-anchor check so a rebuild fires on data refresh / SPA nav / lost anchor and a stale panel from a previous tweet is torn down. Collapse state = `chrome.storage.local['augment:contextCollapsed']` (read once into `contextCollapsed` at `start()` via `initContextCollapsed`; NEVER re-read per render — apply the module var, D39c-style discipline). The `#reply-master-btn{display:none!important}` kill rule is DEFENSIVE only — the real fix is uninstalling the legacy `~/newme/clipx/reply-master` unpacked extension in chrome://extensions (**remind the user at ship**). **NO new tests** — content.ts is untested by convention; the logic is AX.4's bun-tested `tweetContext.ts`. The whole panel is **UNVERIFIED IN A BROWSER** (D64/D65 tail — the unpacked-extension walk isn't scriptable): panel renders/collapses/survives SPA nav, kill rule with the legacy ext enabled-then-removed, and unknown-author muted line + no 404 fetch loop are the live done-when items. **Whole-repo `bun run lint` is now 0 errors** — the 2 pre-existing `Replies.tsx` a11y errors the ME/AI gotchas flagged were fixed by the Wave-1 verification pass `b920507` (`<dialog>` a11y).
