- **GR.4: the People tab is now a two-subtab panel and the curation loop is user-visible — read this before GR.5/GR.6/GR.10 or any People work.** Extension-only, **$0**, **no server/schema/migration/route/test change** (suite stays **1228/0**; UI-only tasks add none by repo convention). **Five things a later task must know:** (1) **`GET /x/following/queue` is a GET that WRITES** — it releases `active→queued` and revokes `queued→active` at read time. `FollowingView` therefore reloads after every PATCH instead of removing rows locally, and nothing may call `api.following.queue()` speculatively (a poll, a prefetch, a mount that isn't the visible subtab) — it would keep topping the batch up to the jittered budget without a human looking at it. The Today tab polls the brief on open/refresh, so if a monitor query ever gets expensive, this is the surface that pays for it, not `GET /x/monitor`. []`. (3) **`key={a.rule}` is only safe because the monitor emits at most one alert per rule** (D103a). If a future rule ever returns two alerts, the card breaks before anything else does. - **GR.8: the brief is now a GET that WRITES, and that is the one line to read before touching `brief.ts` again.** **$0**, no schema/migration/route/MCP change; suite 1311→**1319** (+8: 2 pure quest, 6 brief route). 9 files (the plan said 5; the extra 4 are the D102a extension shape — type mirror, typed client, the second panel surface, one small CSS block). **Seven things a later task must know:** (1) **`GET /x/brief` settles goals.** `loadGoalsWithPacing(now)` is the 11th entry of the big `Promise.all`, so GR.7's lazy `active→achieved|missed` flip now fires every time Today is opened or refreshed — the fourth read-that-writes after `/radar/drafts`, `/following/queue` and `/x/goals`, and the first that does it as a side effect of an unrelated surface. It is idempotent and only advances, but nothing may poll `/x/brief` speculatively expecting a pure read. (2) **The quest targets now have two sources.** `repliesTarget` = an ACTIVE `replies` commitment's `dailyTarget`, else `doctrine.replyTargetMin`; `originalsTarget` = an active `originals` one, else 1. A **paused** row changes nothing but still ships (the panel shows what is paused). `replyQuota.target` is untouched (D111). (3) **`QuestInputs.originalsTarget?` is additive and the `original` label pluralizes only above 1** — `'1 original post'` stays byte-identical, which is what keeps every pre-GR.8 quest test and every streak already on the books valid. A commitment's floor is `MIN_DAILY_TARGET`=1, so it can only ever raise a bar. (4) **Test hygiene is bidirectional with `goals.test.ts`.** `brief.test.ts`'s new describe deletes `commitments` at the start of every test AND in `afterAll`, because `goals.test.ts` blanket-deletes the same table and asserts a fresh commitment carries zero debt — a leaked active row from the brief suite would fail a different file, and file order (b before g) means the leak flows that way. Its goal fixture is `kind:'custom'` precisely so the pacing arithmetic is seeded end-to-end and cannot move with other suites' `account_snapshots`; the debt assertion is baseline-relative (`trackedLast7 === 3` is calendar arithmetic, `missedLast7` is only bounded) because the streak diary is shared. (5) **A goal that settles on the read vanishes from Today with no celebration** — the block is ACTIVE-only by design (a finished goal belongs to the Me tab's ledger). If UI.12 wants a "just achieved" ribbon it needs a recently-settled window; that is a new decision, not a bug. (6) **`goals`/`commitments` are OPTIONAL in the extension's `Brief` type** (the S0.1/GR.6 precedent) and `GoalsCard` returns null on absent-or-empty, so a deployed server predating GR.8 degrades instead of crashing. The Settings editor likewise swallows its load error and leaves the fields blank rather than inventing a target — a save then reports the real error. (7) **Debt copy tiers live in `Today.tsx`, not the server** (presentation): tier 0 renders nothing at all — the C9 no-guilt contract — tier 1 is a `.status-line`, tier ≥2 is `.warn`, and tier 3 points at lowering the bar in Settings rather than scolding. **Browser-UNVERIFIED** (panel React is untested by convention): the live tail is the plan's own done-when — set a replies commitment of 15 in Settings, see the quest label change on Today, and see a followers/custom goal render its pacing bar. **Releases `src/x/routes/brief.ts`, `Today.tsx`, `Settings.tsx`.** Gates: `bun run test` **1319/0**, root + extension typecheck clean, `cd extension && bun run build` green, whole-repo `bun run lint` clean. **GR.10 now also owes `docs/settings-tab.md` (the commitments editor) and another `docs/today-tab.md` pass (Goals card + debt line).**
- **GR.9: the digest now GRADES the week, and generating one WRITES — read this before GR.10 or anything touching `digest.ts`.** **$0** (rides the existing weekly Grok call), no schema/migration/route/MCP change; suite 1319→**1325** (+6: 3 pure, 3 route). 8 files (the plan said 4 — D112d). **Seven things a later task must know:** (1) **`GET /x/digest` is the fifth read-that-writes**, after `/radar/drafts`, `/following/queue`, `/x/goals` and `/x/brief`: the scorecard's `goalVerdicts` come from `loadGoalsWithPacing(now)`, which applies GR.7's lazy `active→achieved|missed` flip. The **cached path returns before `loadFacts` runs**, so re-opening Sunday's digest doesn't re-flip — but `?refresh=true`, `?factsOnly=true` **and the MCP `x_digest` tool (which hard-codes factsOnly)** all do. (2) **The grade is computed in the PURE layer from the facts it grades** (D112a) — if you add a component, feed it from inside `buildDigestFacts` where the number already exists, and only add a `scorecardInputs` field for something no row in the digest can answer. (3) **`repliesPosted`/`replyPct` are `posts_published`-with-`is_reply`, so they include thread self-replies AND depend on the 03:00 UTC discovery pass** — a manually pasted reply only enters `posts_published` when the daily pass finds it on the timeline, so a digest generated for the *current* week under-counts replies until the next pass. That is the same population `activity.replyPct` and the brief's week ratio have always used, which is why both scorecard components read it: moving one of them to `reply_drafts` without the other would grade two different populations against each other. (4) **Two goal loads per fresh digest are deliberate** — `loadGoals` (ME.5's `goals` fact, via `resolveGoals`) and `loadGoalsWithPacing` (the verdicts). Folding them would re-source a shipped fact's numbers from `goalPacing`; check that `resolveGoals`'s `pct` and `goalPacing`'s `pctComplete` actually agree before anyone tries. (5) **Every scorecard constant lives in `src/x/goals.ts`** (`SCORECARD_WEIGHTS` 30/20/25/15/10, `SCORECARD_MIN_DAYS` 4, `RATIO_PENALTY_PER_PCT` 2, `VERDICT_SCORE`) and all of them are **opening guesses** — recalibrate there in one line, never by adding arithmetic to `digest.ts`. (6) **Test-suite traps:** the digest route suite now seeds **12 `streaks` rows across three far-past Mondays** (2025-11-03/-17/-24) plus a `digests` row at 2025-10-27 whose facts carry `scorecard.score = 60` — the delta test asserts `delta === score − 60`, so that seeded row IS the fixture, and all 13 are deleted in `afterAll`. Only `questAdherence` is asserted exactly (60 = 3 all-done of 5); every other component reads rows other suites own, so the score is asserted as a 0–100 number (D103e). (7) **The `digests` table stores facts as JSON**, so every pre-GR.9 cached week simply has no `scorecard` key — `loadPrevScore` reads `facts.scorecard?.score ?? null` and the panel's type mirrors it as `scorecard?: … | null`. **Browser-UNVERIFIED and the live tail is a real Sunday** — nobody has seen a real grade; the first one lands when a week with ≥4 tracked days is generated, and the first *delta* one week after that. Gates: `bun run test` **1325/0**, root + extension typecheck clean, `cd extension && bun run build` green, whole-repo `bun run lint` clean, **`bun scripts/smoke-c9.ts` re-run against the real `./stratus.db` → SMOKE PASS, $0**. **GR.10 now also owes `docs/today-tab.md` a third pass** (the digest grade badge, on top of GR.6's Account-health card and GR.8's Goals card + debt line).
- **GR.7: there is ONE goals system and `/x/me/goals` is its writer — read this before GR.8/GR.9 or anything touching `me_goals`.** Server-only apart from two extension type mirrors; suite 1271→**1311** (+40). **Seven things a later task must know:** (1) **`GET /x/goals` is a GET that WRITES** (the third one, after `/radar/drafts` and `/following/queue`): `loadGoalsWithPacing` applies `active→achieved|missed` guarded by `eq(status,'active')`, so it is idempotent and only advances — but it means a poll or a speculative prefetch silently settles goals. GR.8's brief calls the same loader, so the flip will now happen on every Today open. (2) **`GET /x/me` deliberately does NOT flip** — it lists whatever is stored, which is the pre-GR.7 behaviour; the Me tab can therefore show `active` on a goal `/x/goals` would settle. That is a choice, not an oversight: `/x/me` runs on the draft path through `renderFor` and a read that writes has no business there. (3) **The followers rate query has NO upper bound** (`snapshotAt >= now − 7d`, oldest first). Real snapshots are never future-dated, and the open end is what lets a year-3000 fixture exercise the arithmetic without seeding present-week rows that break suites asserting exact follower numbers. **`people.test.ts` inserts undated (= now) snapshots and never deletes them**, so any assertion about which row wins "oldest in window" is file-order dependent — the route test asserts only that the rate is measured. (4) **Flow goals count from `baseline_at ?? created_at`**, so a pre-GR.7 goal edited into a counted kind measures from its creation, not from all of history. (5) **`COMMITMENT_QUEST_KEY` is the only place the `originals`→`original` mismatch is resolved** (the C9 diary uses the singular quest key). A new commitment key must be added there or its debt reads "missed every day". (6) **A fresh commitment always reads zero debt** (`active_since` = now, window ends yesterday) — that is correct, not a wiring failure; back-date `active_since` to see debt in a manual test. (7) **`computeScorecard` is built, tested and unused** — GR.9 wires it; its inputs were chosen to match the digest facts that already exist (`quests.daysAllDone/daysTracked`, days-with-an-original, `activity.replyPct`, doctrine `weekReplyTargetPct`, goal verdicts), so GR.9 should need no new query beyond the goals loader. **Nothing here has been seen by a human in a browser** — the only UI change is the Me tab's kind dropdown gaining two options; the real surface is GR.8's. Gates: `bun run test` **1311/0**, root + extension typecheck clean, `cd extension && bun run build` green, whole-repo `bun run lint` clean.

- **prior (A3.6 — $0, no schema/migration/route/MCP/extension change; suite 1385→1395, 3 files):** the manual-post reconcile in the daily pass. ### Deviations D114–D126, verbatim

- **D114** (A3.1): **Task 1's edit list (extension-only `extension/src/shared/activeTimes.ts`) was overridden by the same plan's Task 4 line 221 + §7.27** — the pure core (grid types, `parseHeatColors`, `audienceScoreFor`, `gridsEqual`, bounds) lives canonically in `src/shared/activeTimes.ts`, the extension file is the DOM extractor + re-export shim (replyBand arrangement; root tsconfig has no DOM lib, so the split is also forced by typecheck). **Binds A3.2** (import `ACTIVE_TIMES_COLS/MIN_ROWS/MAX_ROWS` from `src/shared/activeTimes.ts` for route validation), **A3.3** (import path is `./shared/activeTimes.ts`, unchanged from plan text) and **A3.4** (the server-side `audienceScoreFor` import the plan asks for needs NO file move — it already lives server-side). Also an interpretation call inside `parseHeatColors`: the plan's per-cell "luminance-or-alpha scalar" became **grid-level alpha mode** (one translucent cell ⇒ alpha carries the signal for ALL cells, so the a=1 peak of an alpha ramp scores 1 instead of flipping to its hue's ~0.53 luminance) — plan intent ("relative intensity is what we need") over letter.

- **D116** (A3.4): **two plan-vs-repo divergences, both resolved in the repo's favour.** (a) Task 4's Read-first + Edit list name `src/x/routes/brief.test.ts` for the `annotateGaps` test, but `annotateGaps` is tested in **`src/test.test.ts`** (the grab-bag pure-function suite, codemap §3.1) — the audienceScore case landed there beside the existing S0.4 block, not in the route suite. (b) The plan calls `audienceScore` "display data for the TodayPlan row", implying Today renders it — but Task 4's edit list contains no `Today.tsx` and no `BriefGap` extension-mirror change, and nothing consumes the field yet. Both were **deliberately left unbuilt** (masterplan "don't pre-stub API surface"): the server now returns `AnnotatedGap.audienceScore`, and whichever later task first renders it (a Today polish, or **A3.14**'s week board, which already sits on this `/brief` payload) adds the `BriefGap` mirror field + the JSX then. No forward task is blocked — a mirror gaining a field it now needs is a one-line hand-sync (the standing Settings-mirror gotcha).
- **D115** (A3.3): **Task 3's edit list said 1 file (content.ts); the mandated live pass (the A3.1 gotcha) forced 3.** The live x.com/i/account_analytics/audience palette carries intensity as ELEMENT `opacity` (0.2..1) over one constant `background-color: hsl(var(--color-blue-500))` — the shipped `cellColor` returned that unresolvable `var()` string for every cell (100% unparseable → capture could never fire), and even the computed color is the same constant `rgb(30,156,241)` for all cells. Fix landed in the DOM half `extension/src/shared/activeTimes.ts` (the file whose header says "adjust live in A3.3"): `cellColor` resolves `var()` backgrounds via computed style and folds element opacity into an rgba alpha (`withOpacity`), which the pure core's existing alpha mode reads correctly — `src/shared/activeTimes.ts` untouched, +2 fixtures. Second live divergence: the metric combobox (`BUTTON[role="combobox"]`, the Audience tab's only one) sits OUTSIDE every ≤8-hop ancestor of the section heading, so `readActiveTimesMetric` in content.ts walks first, then falls back to a page-level `document.querySelector`. **Affects no future task's contract** — the wire shape and the pure core are unchanged.

- **D117** (A3.5): **three implementation decisions the plan text under-specified, all recorded here because two bind future tasks.** **(a) The grep-verify found two `'pending'` enumerations that POST-DATE the plan** (written 2026-07-17; GR.5/GR.6 shipped 2026-07-23/24): `monitor.ts::loadMonitorInputs`' scheduleCluster slot select and `calendar.ts::scheduleWarnings` (fires only for `pending` rows and reads only `pending` neighbors). Both **deliberately stay pending-only at A3.5**: nothing can create a `manual` row before A3.7 ships the Composer toggle, so there is zero regression window — and STATE already earmarks calendar advisory polish to A3.7/A3.14 (the D105 note). **Binds A3.7:** when the toggle lands, extend `scheduleWarnings` to treat `manual` as first-class (fire for manual rows AND include manual neighbors in its select) and consider the one-line `inArray` widening in `loadMonitorInputs` (message wording says "pending" — check `monitor.ts`'s cluster message text); a cadence warning that can't see the manual half of the calendar is wrong the moment manual rows exist. **(b) Plan gap closed: the PATCH promotion path could mint a manual THREAD member** (threads-create rejects manual, but PATCHing a head/tail to `manual` would strand the tails — mark-posted flips one row and the publisher drives tails off a `pending` head only). `PATCH {status:'manual'}` on a row with a `threadId` now 400s `manual_threads_unsupported`, same string as the create door. **(c) `scheduled_for_required_when_pending` is reused verbatim for manual** (no new error string): no client matches it, the semantics are identical, and a second string would double the guard surface for zero information.

- **D118** (A3.6): **two plan-vs-repo divergences in the reconcile, both resolved in the repo's favour.** **(a) `scheduled_posts.id` is a text uuid, not a number** — the plan's matcher signature `{id, ...}` implied a numeric id; `matchManualRows`'s `id`/`scheduledPostId` and `ReconcileLink.scheduledPostId` are therefore **strings**. Any future caller of the matcher passes string ids. **(b) Typed `gte(col, Date)`, not "`.getTime()` in raw sql"** — the plan (Task 6 + this task's earlier next-up) prescribed the raw-sql `.getTime()` bind (§7.13). But §7.13's raw-sql-Date ban only applies to `` sql`` `` templates; the 30-d bounds here are plain Drizzle operators (`inArray`/`isNull`/`isNotNull`/`gte`) on `timestamp_ms` columns, and dailyMetrics.ts's own `snapshotAccount`/`rereadWinners` (and channels.ts) already bind Date objects through the typed operators, which Drizzle converts via the column mapper. No raw sql was written, so no `.getTime()` was needed. **Binds nothing forward** — a note that "the plan said raw sql" is not a defect to chase.

- **D119** (A3.7, discharges the A3.5-half of D117): **the plan's extension-only Task 7 (5 files) grew to 11 by executing the server follow-through D117 earmarked.** A3.5 deliberately left `calendar.ts::scheduleWarnings` and `monitor.ts::loadMonitorInputs` `pending`-only because no manual row could exist yet; **A3.7 is where the toggle mints them, so both were widened here** — `scheduleWarnings` fires for `manual` and includes manual neighbors (`inArray(['pending','manual'])`), `loadMonitorInputs`' slot select does the same, and both user-facing strings moved off "pending" (`pending post`→`scheduled post`, `pending pair`→`scheduled pair`). **No test asserted the old wording** (calendar matches `within`/`20 min away`; brief checks only `severity`/`typeof message`) — a future reword is likewise free of string-assertion drag. **Two smaller plan-vs-repo calls, both resolved in the repo's favour:** (a) the plan's **`posts.markPosted(id)`** has no home — the scheduled-post client methods hang off `api` directly, so it landed as **top-level `api.markPosted`** next to `remove`; any A3.8/A3.14 caller uses `api.markPosted`, not `api.posts.*`. (b) the plan's **"Today renders a manual chip next to the visual chip"** collapsed to a **title on the existing styled `.badge-manual` status pill** — A3.5 shipped that CSS and widened the brief today-select, so the status pill already IS the manual chip; a second "manual" text chip beside a "manual" status pill would double the word. **Binds A3.8/A3.14:** the Calendar/Today manual affordances (`.badge-manual` pill, `.overdue` tint, the sibling `.mark-posted-btn`, `api.markPosted`) exist now — reuse them, don't re-invent; A3.14's week board owns `calendar.ts`+`Calendar.tsx` next and inherits the manual-first `scheduleWarnings`.

- **D120** (A3.8): **the plan's 6-file Task 8 landed as 8, plus three implementation shapes the plan under-specified.** **(a) `api.ts` was a NO-OP** — Task 8's edit list says "reuse `posts.markPosted` from Task 7 (add here if Task 7 hasn't landed)", but A3.7 landed it top-level as `api.markPosted` (D119), so no api.ts edit; the touched set dropped api.ts and picked up `messages.test.ts` (the guard test) + `styles.css` (the `.manual-card` block). **(b) A SIBLING `syncManualAlarms()`, not an extension of `syncLaunchAlarms()`** — the plan says "the existing launch sync additionally fetches manual rows"; implemented as an independent function (fetch `status=manual`, clear only `stratus-manual:`-prefixed alarms) called alongside the launch sync at all three triggers, with its **own `manualChain`/`enqueueManual`** (the established per-session-key-family chain pattern — `manual:due` is a distinct key from `launch:active`/`launch:replies`, so a shared chain would be false coupling). **(c) No grace, no retry, and a re-fire is DELIBERATE** — `computeManualAlarms` fires at `scheduledFor` exactly (unlike launch's +90 s) and `handleManualFire` schedules no retry alarm; but because a still-`manual` recently-missed row re-enters the sync's `wanted` set and re-fires, `mergeManualDue` dedups by postId (refresh-not-stack) and the notification id `stratus-manual-note:<id>` replaces rather than stacks — so an unposted slot re-nudges at most ~once more inside the 30-min miss window, then the overdue chip takes over. **Binds A3.14** (the week board reuses A3.8's `manual:due`/`ManualPostCard` affordances alongside A3.7's chips) and any future extension alarm work (the two alarm prefixes are namespaced siblings — a third family adds its own prefix + parse fn + an `onAlarm` branch BEFORE the `parseLaunchAlarm` fall-through, never widening `parseLaunchAlarm`).

- **D121** (A3.9, discharges the A3.9-half of D5): **the plan's Task 9 predates the AI layer (written 2026-07-17, before AI.2/AI.6), so four of its instructions were overridden by current code + D5, all resolved in the repo's favour.** **(a) `askGrok` + `XAI_API_KEY`→503 `xai_unconfigured` became `askLLM` + `llmConfigured()`→503 `grok_not_configured`** — decision 8 says the DM route reuses the icebreaker refusal ladder verbatim, and AI.6 already migrated that route to `askLLM`/`llmConfigured()`/`llmErrorPayload` keeping the **`grok_not_configured`** string (the string is now provider-agnostic in meaning; kept byte-identical to icebreakers so the two routes' 503 match). The other DM-specific strings the plan names are kept: 404 **`unknown_person`** (vs icebreaker's `not_found`), 422 **`no_shared_context`**. **(b) The registry key is a 4-file move (RL.4), which the plan doesn't mention** (predates AI.3): `dm` added to `PROMPT_KEYS`+`PROMPT_SPECS` (→12 keys), default `DM_PROMPT_TEMPLATE` from the PURE `people/dm.ts`, `registry.test.ts` key-list/defaultBody assertions, `docs/settings-tab.md` 11→12 + the enumerated list. So the file count is **11, not the plan's 7**. **(c) `buildDmPrompt(grounding, idea, purpose)` gained a trailing `template = DM_PROMPT_TEMPLATE` param** for override support — the exact shape of `buildIcebreakerInput(grounding, template)`; the module stays pure and does inline split/join (importing `renderPrompt` from the registry would cycle registry→dm→registry). **(d) `required: ['{{GROUNDING}}']`, `optional: ['{{IDEA}}','{{PURPOSE}}']`** — GROUNDING is the whole safety contract (an override must keep it), idea/purpose are token-tolerant. **Binds A3.10** (the DM UI consumes `POST /x/dms/draft`→`{id,text,grounding,costUsd}`, `GET /x/dms?handle=`, `PATCH /x/dms/:id`; 422/503 render friendly lines) and is the **template for A3.12** (article prompt — same `askLLM` + `article` registry key + the RL.4 4-file move, D5).

- **D122** (A3.10): **the plan's 4-file Task 10 landed as 5, plus two under-specified shapes.** **(a) The deep-link is dossier-open, not scroll-to-section.** Task 10 says "deep-link via the existing `onOpenPerson` + a scroll/flag". A true focus/scroll would need `onOpenPerson`'s signature widened (`(handle, focus?)`), which threads through `App.tsx` → `Today.tsx` → every call site — out of the 4-file budget and into shared plumbing for a marginal affordance. Resolved per the plan's own overriding "keep DoNext light": the `dm_ready` **draft DM** button just calls `onOpenPerson(item.handle)`, landing the user on the dossier where `DmBox` renders right after Openers. **Binds nothing forward** — if a later polish task (UI.14) wants an actual scroll-to-DM, it owns the App/Today signature change then. **(b) styles.css is the 5th file** — the DM box needs CSS to not read as broken (the A3.7/A3.8 precedent, D119/D120, where an extension-only task legitimately adds a `styles.css` block); a compact `.dm-*` block after the icebreaker block, tokens only. **(c) No unit tests** — the plan says "none new (UI over tested routes); browser check is the gate", so `DmBox`/`dmError` are panel-convention browser-verified (like every panel since UI.9), not `bun:test`'d. The suite stays 1433. **Browser-verify owed** (2-min user check over the loaded extension, `./scripts/deploy.sh` + chrome://extensions reload): from a person with real shared context, Draft DM with a Romanian idea → English draft, edit, Copy, Mark sent → the timeline gains a `✉ manual_dm_logged` row; a thin dossier renders the 422 line before any spend. **Also recovered at Step 0:** `extension/src/sidepanel/People.tsx` was found truncated to 0 bytes in the working tree (mtime mid-session, not this task's doing; HEAD intact at 1056 lines, session started clean) → `git restore` from HEAD before editing. If a future session sees a 0-byte tracked file that git reports "modified", restore from HEAD rather than treating it as intentional.

- **D123** (A3.11): **the plan's Task 11 predates all the Wave-4 migrations (written 2026-07-17), so several details were resolved against current code — none change a forward contract, but two bind A3.12/A3.13.** **(a) Migration is `0023_slimy_night_thrasher`, not the plan's Design-header `0013`+** — journal is at `0022`; ignore hardcoded numbers (the standing rule). Clean CREATE TABLE + 1 index, no seed. **(b) Added `articles_status_updated_idx` on `(status, updated_at)` — the Design DDL omitted any index, but every sibling table (ideas/dm_drafts/audience_activity) indexes and the list query is exactly `WHERE status ORDER BY updated_at DESC`.** **(c) The discarded-freeze is a 409 `discarded_locked`, not a silent drop** — the plan says "nothing edits a discarded row except status back to draft"; implemented as: current `discarded` + body is anything other than a bare `{status:'draft'}` → 409 (the dm_drafts terminal-status precedent, §7.10). **(d) `publishedAt` stamps ONLY on the transition into published** (`existing.status !== 'published'`) — so published→draft keeps the stamp as history and a re-publish re-stamps; the plan just says "status:'published' stamps publishedAt". **(e) `publishedUrl` is a decoupled field** (accepted on any PATCH, validated ≤400 → null on empty), not gated to the publish action — keeps autosave flexible; a draft carrying a URL is harmless. **(f) Default list status is `all`** (no filter when `?status=` omitted) — there is no single "active" status for articles (draft AND published are both live), so the ideas.ts `'open'` default has no analog; `?status=draft|published|discarded|all` all work. **(g) `outline` is `text({mode:'json'}).$type<unknown>()`** — the assist's outline JSON shape is A3.12's to define; A3.11 accepts any object/array or null (400 `invalid_outline` otherwise). **Binds A3.12:** the assist route goes in the SAME `src/x/routes/articles.ts`, `outline` mode does `PATCH`-style persist into `articles.outline`, and it reuses `UUID_RE`/`parsePillarField`/the discarded guard rather than re-deriving. **Binds A3.13:** the Writer reads the lean list (`bodyChars`, no `body_md`) for its rail and `GET /articles/:id` for the editor; autosave is `PATCH` partials.

- **D124** (A3.12, discharges the A3.12-half of D5): **the plan's Task 12 predates the AI layer (written 2026-07-17), so five instructions were overridden by current code + D5/D121, all resolved in the repo's favour.** **(a) `askGrok`/`XAI_API_KEY`→503 became `askLLM` + `llmConfigured()`→503 `grok_not_configured`** — the exact A3.9 DM migration (D121a), string kept byte-identical to icebreakers/dms so the three 503s match; `llmErrorPayload` maps upstream 429/502 the same way. **(b) The persona is HARDCODED in `article prompt.md`, NOT `{{PERSONA}}`/`{{BELIEFS}}` niche-substituted** — the plan's tail enumerates only `{{PILLARS}}`/`{{WINNERS}}`/`{{GUIDANCE}}`/`{{ARTICLE}}`/`{{INSTRUCTION}}` (5, no persona), and decision 11 "persona is the product, keeping the full §1 biography" reads as "put the real biography in the prompt" (the DM precedent hardcoded too). Consequence: **the whole prefix is byte-static, so `prompt.cacheKey` alone buckets it — NO `:slug:updatedAt` niche suffix** (unlike post/thread). If a future task wants articles to follow niche switches, it adds `{{PERSONA}}`/`{{BELIEFS}}` as `optional` placeholders + the niche wiring + the cache-key suffix then. **(c) `promptCacheKey:'stratus-article'` (plan) → the registry's `prompt.cacheKey`** (per-body sha; a customized prompt must not share a cached prefix). **(d) `topWinners` was ALREADY exported** (drafter.ts:549), so the plan's "export `topWinners` if private" edit is a NO-OP (the A3.9 api.ts precedent) — file count is **8, not the plan's 7** (drafter.ts dropped; the RL.4 registry 4-file move — registry.ts + registry.test.ts + `docs/settings-tab.md` 12→13 — added, which the plan predated). **(e) Per-mode required seeds the plan left all-optional:** outline/full 400 `idea_required`, section 400 `heading_required`, polish 400 `selection_required` (refuse-before-spend — each mode needs its seed; the plan's `{mode, idea?, heading?, selection?}` type is loose but the semantics demand one per mode). **Also:** a **discarded** article → **409 `discarded_locked`** before the key check (reuse the A3.11 freeze; assist would otherwise spend then write `outline` on a frozen row); the paid happy path is **not unit-tested** (the A3.9 convention — force both keys off → 503; the outline-persist + real drafting are A3.15's `--live` smoke). **Binds A3.13** (the Writer consumes `POST /x/articles/:id/assist` → `{mode, proposal, persisted, costUsd}`; outline is already persisted server-side, the other three modes the editor inserts + autosaves) and **A3.15** (docs — `docs/` still has no Writer doc; the assist route + the 13th prompt key need documenting there).

- **D125** (A3.13): **five plan-vs-repo calls on the Writer page, all resolved in the repo's favour; two bind A3.14/A3.15.** **(a) The `GET /writer` mount needed NO `index.ts` edit and NO sibling router.** Task 13's edit list offers "`data.ts` (or a tiny sibling router)" + implies wiring in `mountX`; but the `explorer` Hono router is ALREADY `app.route('/', explorer)` (index.ts:126), so `GET /writer` was added as a second route on that same router in `data.ts` — root-mounted, outside the /x guard (§7.21), lazy `Bun.file`-cached exactly like `/explorer`. So the touched set is 4 files (`public/writer.html`, `data.ts`, `data.test.ts`, `smoke-explorer.ts`), never `index.ts`. **(b) Smoke: the plan's "extend `smoke-explorer.ts` OR leave for Task 15 — choose one" was resolved to EXTEND NOW** — a `/writer` serves-the-shell assertion is in `smoke-explorer.ts`, plus a `bun:test` in `data.test.ts` (the "one-line test beside the explorer's"). **Binds A3.15: its smoke must NOT re-add the `/writer` shell assertion** (already covered). **(c) Full-draft overwrite uses a dialog-free inline confirm, not native `confirm()`** — consistent with the repo's no-blocking-dialog posture; Delete is likewise a two-click arm-then-confirm. **(d) The client mirrors two server behaviours locally rather than re-fetching:** the assist response returns `{proposal}` NOT the updated row, so after an OUTLINE the page fills title/subtitle only-while-default (the server's own rule) and inserts the skeleton, then autosaves (idempotent); SECTION passes the section's own notes (heading→next-heading) as `selection`. **(e) `GET /x/pillars?active=true` returns raw `content_pillars` rows — EMPTY for a fresh builder DB** (the `DEFAULT_PILLARS` fallback lives in `getActivePillars()`, which the route does NOT use; only the article POST/PATCH validation does). The dropdown handles empty gracefully. **Binds A3.15 (docs):** `docs/` still has no Writer doc — A3.15 creates it, and it must cover the `/writer` page itself (token-key sharing with the explorer, the MD editor/autosave, the four assists, Copy-for-X rich-vs-plain outcome) on top of A3.11/A3.12's CRUD+assist+discarded-freeze+no-URL-surcharge facts.

- **D126** (A3.14): **four plan-vs-repo calls on the week board, all resolved in the repo's favour; two bind A3.15.** **(a) The signature grew and returns a richer object.** Task 14's `buildWeekBoard(now, posts, cells, audience): DayColumn[]` became `buildWeekBoard(now, posts, drafts, cells, audience): {columns, tray}` + two exported helpers `occupiedSlotDates(posts)` and `slotDateFor(dayStart, hour, rand=Math.random)` — the plan's OWN tests demand pure "drafts-tray ordering" and "scheduling target date math", and it mandates all assembly live in `calendarLogic.ts` (component renders view-models only). So drafts + the ghost-click Date math are pure/tested, not component-side. **(b) "Claimed anchor" was undefined — occupancy = `pending|manual|posted|publishing`** (draft/failed/cancelled free the slot). Used for BOTH the ghost gaps and the "→ best slot" `suggestBestSlotDate` call, so the board and the scheduler agree on what's taken (an improvement over the Composer's pending-only `readSlottedPending`; the board avoids ALL slot-holders). **(c) A 0-intensity audience hour is NOT tagged 'audience'.** `slotHint(cell, audienceScore)` (reused verbatim, the §7.19 authority) returns 'audience' for ANY non-null score including 0 — so with a capture present EVERY unmeasured ghost would read "aud", including dead hours. The board gates the hint on `audienceScore > 0` (the `audiencePeakHours` "dead hours aren't peaks" philosophy) while still exposing the raw `audienceScore` on the ghost. `slotHint` itself is untouched — the gate is board-local. **(d) The D105 PATCH-warn item was NOT A3.14's.** STATE's `calendar.ts` lock note said "PATCH still does NOT warn — the one remaining A3.14 follow-up on this file", but Task 14's Edit list is extension-only (3 files, no `calendar.ts`) and its Done-when never mentions warnings. The board's "→ best slot"/ghost-click PATCHes a draft to `pending`/`manual` through `PATCH /posts/scheduled/:id`, which computes NO `scheduleWarnings` (only POST-create does) — the same advisory gap the Composer's edit-schedule already has. Left as-is: it is out of Task 14's scope, not a regression the board introduced in kind. **Binds A3.15** (the docs owe a Writer doc + the week board + the manual/DM/composer surfaces — see next-up) and any future task wanting PATCH-time schedule warnings (an unclaimed server item on `calendar.ts`, now released — the D105 note is retired here).

### Gotchas (A3.1–A3.14), verbatim

- **A3.1: the luminance DIRECTION of an opaque Active-times palette is theme-dependent and unverified.** `parseHeatColors` assumes higher scalar = more activity. Alpha ramps (the likely live palette — X blue at varying opacity) are immune: alpha unambiguously rises with intensity. But an OPAQUE light-theme palette runs pale→dark (more activity = LOWER luminance), which would invert the grid. **A3.3's live pass must eyeball one real capture against the on-page heatmap** and, if inverted, flip the luminance branch in `src/shared/activeTimes.ts` (one line) — do not "fix" it by re-normalizing downstream. Also live-verify the two structural guesses in `extractActiveTimesSection`: cells are inline-background LEAF divs, and the layout is either 7 parent groups or one flat rows-of-7 container — every selector/hop is a named constant at the top of `extension/src/shared/activeTimes.ts`.
- **A3.1: `extension/tsconfig.app.json` include is now a 4-entry array** (`src/**/*` + the three out-of-tree canonicals). A future shared module needs its canonical path appended there or `cd extension && bun run typecheck` fails on the out-of-tree import — the error names the file, but only if you run that gate (root typecheck won't see it).
- **A3.2: the `/x/analytics/active-times` GET returns the FULL DB row (incl. `id`) under `capture`, and `?history=` returns `{count, captures}` newest-first** — A3.4's Composer blending and any extension type mirror should model `{capture: AudienceCapture | null}` off that, not the plan's field list. A3.3 needs NO server-side suppression: the route is append-only and a duplicate POST is harmless by design; the 12h/`gridsEqual` gate lives client-side in `chrome.storage.local`. Also: whole-repo `bun run lint` is now **0 errors** (the two `Replies.tsx` a11y stragglers noted at D11/D13 are gone — fixed somewhere in Wave 3; per-task gate stays "changed files lint clean").
- **GR.10 lint trap (new, and it cost a round-trip):** biome's `useTemplate` + `noUnusedTemplateLiteral` reject a `console.log` built as `` `a ${x}` + 'b' `` or `` `plain` + `b ${x}` `` — a concatenation is only accepted when **every** operand is a template literal that actually interpolates (that is why `smoke-passive-harvest.ts` passes with the same shape). `bunx biome check --write` will NOT fix it; the fixes are classed unsafe. Run `bunx biome check --fix --unsafe <file>` on a new script, then re-check.
- **A3.3: the live Active-times DOM, verified 2026-07-24 (all via in-page probes, x.com/i/account_analytics/audience):** the grid is a FLAT rows-of-7 row-major CSS grid, 7×24 (hourly rows — not the plan's guessed 48; `audienceScoreFor`'s 24-row passthrough covers it), H2 heading, found at ancestor hop 1; it renders TWICE — a hidden responsive twin PRECEDES the visible grid in DOM order and `collectGrid` picks it, which is safe because both twins carry identical per-cell opacities (verified equal, all 168). The visible grid's container has 8 grid tracks (7 day columns + a 32.5px hour-rail track) but the rail labels aren't background-styled leaves, so the 168-group stays clean. Sub-tab navigation rewrites `location.pathname` under the same `/i/account_analytics` prefix (`/overview`, `/audience`) without reloads.
- **A3.3: the `parseHeatColors` luminance branch is still direction-unverified** — the live palette is an opacity ramp (alpha mode), so the luminance path is only reachable if X ships an OPAQUE palette someday; the A3.1 warning (pale→dark light-theme palette would invert the grid) transfers to whoever first sees a capture that disagrees with the on-page heatmap. The alpha half of the A3.1 gotcha is RESOLVED — direction confirmed correct against the live page (Sa hottest column reproduced).
- **A3.3 verification debt (2-minute user check, not a code risk):** the loaded-extension end-to-end (visit Audience → one row in `audience_activity`; revisit <12h → no new row; Settings passiveCapture off → no capture) needs `./scripts/deploy.sh` (prod predates A3.2's route) + a chrome://extensions reload of the unpacked extension — neither is automatable from a session. Everything upstream of the POST (path gate, extraction, palette parse, payload shape, metric read) was verified live in-page; the transport is the byte-tested flushSightings idiom.
- **A3.4: `suggestBestSlotDate`'s `audience` is the SIXTH arg, after `rand`.** A caller that wants blending must pass `rand` explicitly — the Composer does `suggestBestSlotDate(now, slotted, bestCells, SLOT_HORIZON_DAYS, Math.random, audience)`. Every pre-A3.4 5-arg call is untouched and behaves identically (audience defaults undefined → all audienceScores null → tier 2 collapses to earliest). Same shape on the server: `annotateGaps(gapHours, cells, localWeekday, audience?)`.
- **A3.4: the full `audience_activity` DB row is fed straight to `audienceScoreFor` as an `ActiveTimesGrid`** — it is a structural superset (extra `id`/`capturedAt` are ignored), so `brief.ts` does `const audienceGrid: ActiveTimesGrid | null = audienceRows[0] ?? null` with no cast and the Composer passes its `AudienceCapture` state directly. If a future change narrows the select's columns, keep `cols`/`rows`/`grid`/`tzOffsetMin`/`metric` or the assignment stops typechecking. `AudienceCapture.capturedAt` is an **ISO string** over the wire (a `timestamp_ms` Date serialized by `c.json`), which is why the Composer's staleness check is `new Date(audience.capturedAt).getTime()`.
- **A3.5: `mark-posted` returns the row with `postedTweetId` still null** — linking is the A3.6 reconcile's job (decision 6); a client wanting the tweet id after marking waits for the daily pass. A3.7's Calendar "mark posted" action and A3.8's Today card should render off `status`, never off `postedTweetId`.
- **A3.5/A3.7: calendar-fixture day bands are now spoken for:** GR.6 advisory suite 90–130d out, A3.5 manual suite 150–160d, brief monitor pair ~200d, **A3.7 manual-warns-near-pending pair ~300d** (`calendar.test.ts`'s second describe). A future suite seeding `scheduled_posts` with far-future `scheduledFor` picks a fresh band ≥45 min (SCHEDULE_CLUSTER_MS) clear of all four, and any TODAY-window calendar fixture must either be non-`pending`/non-`manual` or clean up before brief/monitor assertions run (brief.test's A3.5 manual fixture is the sanctioned shape). **Note since A3.7:** `scheduleWarnings` now counts BOTH `pending` and `manual` neighbors — a `manual` fixture within 45 min of a `pending` one now cross-warns where it wouldn't before, and `nearDuplicate`/pendingTwin has NO time window (identical text in any band cross-matches). The A3.5 suite's two `URL_TEXT` manual rows (at 150 d + 152 d) now produce a harmless pendingTwin warning on the second create; no assertion checks it, so it's fine — but a NEW manual fixture reusing an existing text will warn.
- **A3.7: `nextEditStatus` is a Composer-local pure helper with NO unit test** — the plan only mandated the cost-estimate test, and the extension convention is panels-are-browser-verified. Its edit-transition table (manual↔API↔draft↔pending over a single row) is covered by typecheck + the browser-verify debt below, not bun:test. If a later task needs to assert it, extract it or exercise it through a route round-trip (there's no composerLogic home for it — it references `PostStatus`, which lives in `shared/types.ts`).
- **A3.7 browser-verify debt (2-minute user check over the LOADED extension, not a code risk):** the whole manual-publish UI is panel-convention unverified — (a) Composer: the API|Manual toggle appears for single posts only (hidden in thread mode), Manual auto-selects when editing a `media_note` row, the cost line reads `$0 · you paste it`, the URL warning is gone, submit is blocked until a time is set, and a manual create lands a `manual` row; (b) Calendar: the manual row shows the pill + an amber `.overdue` tint once its slot passes, and **Mark posted** flips it to `posted` then the chip updates on reload; (c) Today: the manual pill's paste-hint title. Needs `./scripts/deploy.sh` (prod predates A3.5's route) + a chrome://extensions reload. Everything server-side (warnings over manual, mark-posted transition, cost math) is unit-tested.
- **A3.6: `reconcileManualPosts` runs on EVERY daily pass and on every `POST /posts/reconcile`, and it is a WRITE that never re-fires per row.** It selects `scheduled_posts` `status IN ('manual','posted')` with `posted_tweet_id IS NULL` — so once a row is linked (its `posted_tweet_id` stamped) it drops out of the candidate set permanently; a smoke seeding a manual row + a matching `posts_published` original and calling `reconcileManualPosts` directly (A3.15's plan) sees the link happen ONCE. The matcher is exported pure from `src/x/posts/manualReconcile.ts`; the impure wrapper lives in `dailyMetrics.ts` and is NOT exported (unit-tested via the matcher; the wrapper is smoke-covered per plan). **`RunResult` gained `manualLinked`** — the only consumer is `POST /posts/reconcile` (returns the whole result as JSON), so it's a safe additive field; no test asserts the result shape.
- **A3.6: the reconcile deliberately never touches `posts_published.retired`/`pollCount`/`isReply`, only `scheduled_post_id`** — so it slots between `discover` and `snapshotDue` with zero interaction (both later steps key off `retired`/`pollCount`/`isReply` for their candidate sets). If a future daily-pass step starts reading `scheduled_post_id` as a candidate filter, re-examine the ordering.
- **A3.8: manual reminders are a SIBLING of the C7 launch machinery, not a fork.** `background.ts` now has TWO alarm families with namespaced prefixes (`stratus-launch:`/`stratus-launch-retry:` vs `stratus-manual:`) and TWO session-key chains (`launchChain` for `launch:*`, `manualChain` for `manual:due`); `syncManualAlarms()` runs beside `syncLaunchAlarms()` at every trigger (module-load, `stratus/launch-sync`, the 15-min `LAUNCH_SYNC_ALARM`). A future alarm family copies the shape — its own prefix + `parseX` + an `onAlarm` branch placed **before** the `parseLaunchAlarm` fall-through — and must **never widen `parseLaunchAlarm`** (it would swallow the new family's names). There is deliberately NO retry: `handleManualFire` fires once per alarm; a still-unposted slot re-fires only because the sync re-creates the clamped alarm inside the 30-min miss window, and `mergeManualDue`'s postId dedup keeps the card single.
- **A3.8: the pure core in `extension/src/shared/launch.ts` is fully unit-tested; `ManualPostCard.tsx` is browser-verify-only** (panel convention — no bun test, like every panel since UI.9). The card READS `manual:due` from `chrome.storage.session` and **must not write it** (§7.24 single-writer): Copy is local, but Mark posted and dismiss both send `stratus/manual-dismiss {postId}` and let the background mutate the key. It renders off the stored entry / `status`, **never `postedTweetId`** (still null until the A3.6 daily reconcile links the pasted tweet). `computeManualAlarms`/`mergeManualDue` reuse the existing `SchedulablePost`/`LaunchAlarm` types (not new ones) — a manual alarm IS just `{name, when}`.
- **A3.9: `loadIcebreakerGrounding(handle)` is now exported from `routes/people.ts`, NOT a `people/*` module** — it needs `db` + the people/voice/channel selects, so it lives in the route file, and `routes/dms.ts` does a **route→route import** (`import { loadIcebreakerGrounding } from './people.ts'`). No cycle (`people.ts` doesn't import `dms.ts`). It returns the `IcebreakerGroundingInputs` object (person + fallbacks + exchanges + saved tweets + shared channels), null ONLY when the person row is missing; the caller then runs `renderIcebreakerGrounding(inputs, now)` which is where the 422 (materially-empty dossier) is decided. If a later task needs grounding elsewhere, import this — never re-assemble it (decision 8).
- **A3.9: `src/x/people/dm.ts` is a PURE module the registry imports — it must never import from `../prompts/registry.ts` or any route** (registry→dm→registry cycles, the RL.4 rule). That is why `buildDmPrompt` does its own inline `split(placeholder).join(value)` (idempotent, $-safe) instead of calling the registry's `renderPrompt`. Placeholders: `{{GROUNDING}}` required, `{{IDEA}}`/`{{PURPOSE}}` optional/token-tolerant; an absent idea/purpose substitutes a labeled sentinel (`(none — …)`) so the model never reads a blank slot as "omit".
- **A3.9: the DM route suite tests only the $0 ladder + pure CRUD; the paid `POST /dms/draft` happy path is NOT unit-tested** (the `routes/icebreakers.test.ts` convention — forcing both provider keys off makes a grounded person hit 503 instead of spending). `routes/dms.test.ts` seeds `dm_drafts` rows DIRECTLY (bypassing the LLM) to exercise `GET`/`PATCH`; the FK means a seeded row needs its `people.handle` to exist first, and handles stay ≤15 chars (the NT.2 rule — a longer one is silently skipped by `normalizePersonHandle` and `safeLogPersonEvents` goes vacuous). The suite clears `personEvents`+`dm_drafts` per test (`afterEach`) so the sent-logging count stays deterministic.
- **A3.9: `dm_drafts` PATCH ratchet — `sent` and `discarded` are terminal (409 `status_locked`), `sent→sent` is an idempotent no-op** (returns the row, no second event), text is editable only while `draft` (else 409 `text_locked`). Marking sent logs `manual_dm_logged` with the deterministic id `manual_dm_logged:dm_drafts:<id>` via `safeLogPersonEvents` (§7.8 best-effort — a log failure never fails the PATCH; `onConflictDoNothing` on the deterministic id keeps it single even if the no-op guard were ever removed). `mark-posted`-style linking does NOT apply here — a DM is not a scheduled post.
- **A3.10: the DM draft's `grounding` field has TWO shapes depending on the endpoint.** `POST /x/dms/draft` returns `grounding` as a **plain string** (the rendered block, in `DmDraftResult`), but a stored `dm_drafts` row (`GET /x/dms`, `PATCH /x/dms/:id`, in `DmDraft`) has `grounding` as the **`{block, idea}` object** (or null). The extension mirrors both separately — don't unify them. `DmBox` shows `draft.grounding` (the string) in the "What it knew" `<pre>`, and never reads the stored object's grounding.
- **A3.10: `DmBox` in `People.tsx` is panel-convention browser-verified, NO unit test** (the plan mandated none). Its `dmError` 422/503 mapping and the Mark-sent "persist edits then flip status" two-PATCH sequence are covered by typecheck + the owed browser-verify, not `bun:test`. If a later task needs to assert them, exercise through the route round-trip (the routes are tested in `routes/dms.test.ts`) — there's no shared pure module to extract into.
- **A3.10: the `dm_ready` "draft DM" button only opens the dossier — there is no scroll-to-DM.** `onOpenPerson`'s signature was deliberately NOT widened (D122a). A UI.14 polish wanting an actual focus/scroll owns the `App.tsx`→`Today.tsx`→call-site signature change; until then the affordance is "land on the dossier, the DM box is right after Openers".
- **A3.10: `People.tsx` now hosts `DmBox` + the "Draft DM" section (binds UI.14).** UI.14 polishes People/Channels/Voice/Ideas and owes `docs/people-tab.md` a re-read — the dossier gained a Draft-DM surface (idea input → draft → Copy/Mark sent/Discard + prior-DMs list) that the doc does not yet describe; A3.15's docs-sync is where `docs/people-tab.md` first documents it (Task 10's own doc line was deferred to Task 15, note-only).
- **A3.11: `articles.ts` PATCH SELECTs the row first, then guards** (unlike `ideas.ts`, which does a blind `UPDATE … WHERE id`). It must — the discarded-freeze and the publish-transition stamp both need the CURRENT status. So a PATCH is two queries (select + update); a 404 comes from the select, not an empty `returning()`. If A3.12's assist route (outline mode) persists into the same row, follow this shape (or reuse the discarded guard) so it can't quietly write a frozen article.
- **A3.11: the `articles.test.ts` ordering assertions SEED rows directly with explicit `updatedAt`** (`new Date(1000)` / `new Date(2000)`) rather than relying on create-order — two POSTs in the same millisecond tie on `updatedAt` and the `newest-first` assertion flakes. Any A3.12/A3.13 test asserting article order must do the same (seed distinct `updatedAt`), not create-and-hope. The pillar tests use the builder-niche default slugs (`ai-craft` via numeric shorthand `1`; `getActivePillars()` falls back to `DEFAULT_PILLARS` for the builder niche in a fresh :memory: DB).
- **A3.11: `docs/` has NO articles/Writer doc yet** — A3.15's docs-sync creates it (the Writer is a `/writer` page, not an extension tab, so it may want a new `docs/writer.md` rather than a `*-tab.md`). The CRUD contract, the discarded-freeze 409, and the "articles never hit the API so no URL surcharge" fact all need documenting there; none is written down outside the codemap + this STATE entry. **A3.12 adds the assist route + the 13th prompt key to that undocumented set** — all three need the Writer doc.
- **A3.12: `article prompt.md` (repo root) ↔ `ARTICLE_PROMPT_TEMPLATE` (`src/x/articles/prompt.ts`) are asserted byte-identical** by `src/x/articles/prompt.test.ts` (`.trimEnd()` compare, `.md` read via `new URL('../../../article prompt.md', import.meta.url)`). **Edit BOTH together** — the standing byte-sync rule. The .md's backtick code-spans (`` `## Heading` ``, the JSON shape examples) are escaped `` \` `` in the TS literal; there is **no `$`** in the prose (a `${` would interpolate) — keep it that way. Adding the `article` key made the registry a **13-key** set; `registry.test.ts`'s `for (const key of PROMPT_KEYS)` loop asserts every default `validatePromptBody`s clean (`unknown:[]`), so **every `{{X}}` in the template must be in `required`∪`optional`** — the 5 tail tokens are all `required`, `optional:[]`.
- **A3.12: the assist route is a route→route import** — `topWinners` from `./drafter.ts` + `loadPostGuidanceSafe` from `./playbook.ts` (no cycle: neither imports `routes/articles.ts`; the dms→people precedent, A3.9). The PURE `articles/prompt.ts` imports `posts/prompt` (WinnerPost type only) + `posts/pillars` (`renderPillars`/`DEFAULT_PILLARS`/`PillarDef`) + `grok/index` (GrokMessage) — **never the registry/a route** (the registry imports IT for the `article` default; a back-import cycles, the RL.4 rule).
- **A3.13: `public/writer.html` is a standalone SERVER-served page, NOT part of the `extension/` build** — so A3.13 was NOT an extension-touching task (no `cd extension && bun run build`/`typecheck` gate). It's vanilla JS with no TypeScript/bundler pass at all: the shell is only ever verified by the `data.test.ts` mount test + `smoke-explorer.ts` (both assert the served HTML) + the owed real-browser check. The routes it calls (`/x/articles`, `/x/articles/:id/assist`, `/x/pillars`) ARE bun-tested, so the wire contract is covered even though the page's JS is not. If a future task edits `writer.html`, there is no compiler to catch a typo — lean on the browser-verify.
- **A3.13: the Writer and the explorer SHARE one localStorage token key (`stratus_explorer_token`)** — deliberate (one paste covers both public pages). A future change to the explorer's token key (UI.16's token-polish is the likely toucher) MUST change `writer.html` in lockstep or the two pages stop sharing auth. Both pages are public-by-design shells on the same root `explorer` router; every DATA fetch still carries the bearer and 401→re-prompt.
- **A3.13: the Writer's autosave is clobber-safe by a SENT-baseline, not an inputs-baseline.** `save()` snapshots the editable fields it is about to PATCH, and on success sets `state.saved` to THAT snapshot (not the live inputs, which may have advanced mid-request), then re-diffs to decide if it's still dirty. It PATCHes only changed fields and **omits an empty title** (the server 400s `invalid_title` on an empty title PATCH — a cleared box keeps the stored title until a non-empty one is typed). Any A3.14/UI task adding another editor-over-PATCH surface should copy this shape rather than re-fetch-and-overwrite.
- **A3.13: Copy-for-X rich clipboard (`ClipboardItem` text/html) needs a SECURE CONTEXT** (https or localhost) — on a plain-http prod host `navigator.clipboard.write` may reject, which is why there is a **plain-text fallback button** and why the plan flags rich paste as the browser-verify risk item. The real-browser check must record which path worked (rich vs plain) in the eventual A3.15/deploy notes.
- **A3.12: outline mode is the ONLY assist that WRITES, and it does NOT reuse the PATCH handler** — after the shared SELECT-then-guard (`existing`, incl. the discarded 409), it does an inline `db.update(articles).set({outline, updatedAt, title?, subtitle?})` — filling `title`/`subtitle` **only while still at their defaults** (`'Untitled'`/null|''), never clobbering a human-set title. section/polish/full return `{mode, proposal, persisted:false, …}` and write nothing (the human keeps the body). A discarded article refuses the whole assist (409) before any spend — revive it first. `DEFAULT_TITLE='Untitled'` is now a shared const in `articles.ts` (the POST handler uses it too).

- **A3.14: `Calendar.tsx` is now a thin renderer over the pure `calendarLogic.ts` — put board logic THERE, not the component.** `buildWeekBoard`/`occupiedSlotDates`/`slotDateFor` are pure (no React/chrome/Date.now — `now`/`rand` injected) and reuse the Composer's `pickAnchors`/`findScheduleGaps`/`bestTimeCellScore`/`slotHint`/`jitterMinutes` by IMPORT — a recalibration of the anchor ladder or the best-time n-gate moves the Composer AND the board at once (don't re-derive). The component is **browser-verify-only** (no bun test, panel convention since UI.9); the pure module has 10 tests. If a later task needs to assert board behavior, exercise `calendarLogic.ts`, not the JSX.
- **A3.14: the board feeds `AudienceCapture` straight into `buildWeekBoard`'s `audience: ActiveTimesGrid | null` param** (the A3.4 pattern — `AudienceCapture` is a structural superset, `id`/`capturedAt` ignored). `api.metrics.bestTimes`/`api.analytics.activeTimes` are loaded with a `.catch(() => null)` inside the load `Promise.all` so a failure just leaves ghosts neutral (the picker degrades to earliest-open) — only `api.list` rejecting fails the load. Chip flags + the 60-char snippet are computed in the pure `BoardRow`; the component still formats the raw `scheduledFor` via `formatTime` (Intl is locale-bound, kept OUT of the tested pure layer — the tests assert on `hint`/`hour`/`overdue`/ids, never formatted strings).
- **A3.14: scheduling from the board goes through `PATCH /posts/scheduled/:id` (`api.update`), which does NOT compute `scheduleWarnings`** (only POST-create does). So a draft placed via "→ best slot" or a ghost click lands silently even if it's <45 min from another post — the D105/D126d gap. A `url_in_text` 400 (a URL scheduled to `pending`) is the ONE content error the board surfaces, caught via `ApiError.code === 'url_in_text'` → a "switch to Manual" inline nudge (a media-note draft is scheduled `manual` and never trips it).

## Archived at UI.11 (2026-07-24) — the Wave-5 registry lane (UI.2–UI.7) + Wave-4 leftovers

STATE.md had reached 105 KB (three `Read` calls at Step 0). Moved verbatim below: the six header
"prior" paragraphs A3.15 → UI.6 (UI.7 stays live as the immediately-prior task), the fully-discharged
pre-seeded adaptations D1–D5/D9/D10, the closing Wave-4 entry D127, the whole registry-lane register
D128–D133, and eleven gotchas whose content either binds nothing open or survives consolidated in
STATE.md's Wave-3/Wave-4/Wave-5 carry-forward entries.

### Header "prior" paragraphs A3.15 → UI.6

- **prior (UI.6 — xhigh, deps UI.1✓+UI.2✓+UI.5✓, $0 [one authorized GET per 5 min while the browser is open], extension + one skill script, no schema/migration/route/MCP change, 16 files [3 new]; suite 1502→1518 [+16]):** the settings moat crossed the wire. `background.ts::syncServerSettings(force)` (the rankmap cache shape) writes `chrome.storage.local['settings:server']` from `GET /x/settings/values?scope=mirrored` — worker start + the 15-min alarm are TTL-guarded, the NEW `stratus/settings-sync` message FORCES (sent once per panel open by `App.tsx`, and by every `settingsClient` write). NEW zero-import `shared/serverSettings.ts` owns the baked fallbacks + `readServerConfig` (per-key fallback, never throws); NEW `sidepanel/serverSettingsHook.ts` reads on mount + `onChanged`. `composerLogic`/`calendarLogic` took a trailing defaulted `cfg` (byte-identical without it); `Composer.tsx`+`Calendar.tsx` pass the SAME object, `Conversations.tsx` derives its budget from `panelRefreshCap`. `md_to_schedule.ts` fetches the ladder with a baked fallback on every failure (Decision 8 done) — verified live against prod plus all 3 fallback branches. **Browser-verify owed** (panel convention): PATCH `anchors3` → one sync → the Composer's Best time AND the Calendar ghosts move. See **D132**.

- **prior (UI.5 — xhigh, dep UI.1✓, $0, server-only, no schema/migration/extension/MCP change, 14 files [1 new]; suite 1495→1502 [+7]):** the money groups. `budgets` (2) + `ai` (6) + `mentions` (3) → **13 groups, 41 knobs**; MCP stays **23**. Standing rule this task adds: **a money knob is read at REQUEST time inside the refuse-before-spend ladder, never captured earlier** — `makeOnCost` takes `dailyBudgetUsd: number | (() => number)` and `startXWorkers` passes a getter (`getDailyBudgetUsd` resolves per read, so the watchdog AND `/cost/today`/`/cost/daily` move on a PATCH; a throwing getter degrades to "no budget", never a 500); `images.ts` reads `x.budgets.imageDailyUsd` per call and **`0` disables generation** (the old env read treated 0 as invalid → $0.50); `routes/mentions.ts` reads cap+page-size before the token fetch (`0` refuses, the 429 reports the CONFIGURED cap); `workers/dailyMetrics.ts` reads the same page size per run. The `x.ai.*` knobs are the **house-default tier `askLLM` merges last** (body > the `ai` blob > these) — the blob's `null` fields already defer to this tier, so no second owner; `replies.ts` exports `replyLlmDefaults()` (single+batch share it; the batch keeps its COMPUTED cap), `parseBatchTweets` takes the cap as a defaulted param, `drafter.ts`/`digest.ts` read theirs inline and their orphaned constants were DELETED. Defaults are today's constants (reply cap **520**, not the plan's 350), floor 300. `pullMax` is a SEPARATE opt from `maxResults` (CA.1). Only `x.mentions.panelRefreshCap` is mirrored. See **D131**.

- **prior (UI.4 — high, dep UI.1✓, $0, server-only, no schema/migration/extension change, 13 files; suite 1486→1495 [+9]):** three registry groups + consumers + the first setting-writing MCP tool. `gates` (`minCellN` 5–100/20 → `routes/playbook.ts`'s DEFAULT gate, `?minN=` still wins per read, **and** `loadReplyGuidance`/`loadPostGuidance`; `bestTimeMinN` 1–20/3, **`mirrored`** → BOTH `routes/metrics.ts` `/metrics/best-times` and `routes/brief.ts`'s cadence gaps via a trailing defaulted `minN` on the pure `annotateGaps`). `radar` (`draftTtlH` 6–168/48 → the lazy expiry; `radarDraftExpired` gained a defaulted `ttlMs`). `workers` (`dailyMetricsHourUtc`/`publisherIntervalSec` **`appliesOn:'restart'`**, read ONCE in `startXWorkers` + logged; `winnerRereadMinViews` [env-defaulted via `envNumberDefault`] / `winnerRereadCap` read at the START of each daily run, `cap<=0` returning before the candidate query AND its claim write). MCP: curated `x_settings` + write `x_update_setting` → **23 tools (3/15/5)**, `src/mcp.test.ts` count + `docs/s2-mcp-server.md`'s three counts + a new security-table row moved in the same commit. Tests: registry groups/ranges/restart-scope + `every registry default validates against its own def`; a 12-sample funnel cell flipping at `minCellN:10` (own last-in-file describe, HV.5 exemplar); a PATCHed best-time gate; a 10h radar draft surviving 48h and dying at 6h; the MCP round-trip incl. the out-of-ceiling 400 surfaced as an error result. See **D130**.

- **prior (UI.3 — high, dep UI.1✓, $0, server-only, no schema/migration/extension/MCP change, 11 files; suite 1477→1486 [+9]):** four registry groups + consumers via the UI.2 pattern. `people` (mutualExchangeDays/allyExchangeDays/allyWindowDays → `computeStage(…, thresholds=STAGE_DEFAULTS)`; **read by `store.ts::recomputePerson` directly** — D129b, the one impure-DB store read, so a threshold is honored in every ingest path, not just a route; ratchet still never demotes). `followups` (chainLiveMaxAgeH/dmReadyWindowDays/neglectedTargetDays/neglectedAllyDays/momentumWeeklyPct/reupMinAgeDays/reupMaxAgeDays/fanUnacknowledgedDays → `classifyFollowups(inputs, windows=FOLLOWUP_DEFAULTS)`, `momentumInflection(…, {weeklyPctThreshold})`, reup-age SQL, `fanUnacknowledged(…, unackDays)` — all in `routes/followups.ts`). `pinned` (staleDays/outperformRatio [fractional, min 1.5, step 0.5] → `brief.ts::buildPinnedWatch(…, {staleDays, outperformRatio})`). `digest` (own `neglectedCap` + reads the `followups` neglected windows as a 2nd consumer — the weekly list moves with the daily queue). **`x.people.targetBandMinX/MaxX` DROPPED** (niche-owned, 3rd D2 application — `voice.ts` untouched). Tests: registry group-order/keys/fractional-range, stage custom-thresholds matrix, followups custom-windows/threshold/fan-days, +1 followups route done-when (`setSettings`/`resetSettings` directly, reset in `finally`). See **D129**.

- **prior (UI.2 — xhigh, dep UI.1✓+N.5✓, D2-bound, $0, server-only, no schema/migration/extension/MCP change, 7 files; suite 1469→1477 [+8] — first Wave-5 task):** the first real consumers of the UI.1 settings store + the D2 ownership call. **D2 resolved Option B:** DROPPED the 3 niche-owned band keys `x.doctrine.{replyTargetMin,replyTargetMax,weekReplyTargetPct}` UI.1 had seeded (the niche owns them via `loadDoctrine`), so each registry knob has exactly one owner; kept only the cadence ladder (`anchors3/anchors4/ladderSwitchAt`) in `doctrine`, added `quests` (`originalsTarget`/`neglectedTargetsCount`/`neglectedTargetDays`/`launchAttendWindowMin`) + `display` (`sparklineDays`/`leaderCount`) groups, all `server` scope (no `x.quests.replyQuestTarget` — the reply quest derives from the niche band). `quests.ts` stays pure (Decision 6): `computeQuests(i, opts=QUEST_DEFAULTS)`, `neglectedTargetsAtDayStart(…, neglectedDays=)`, `launchesAttended(…, windowMs=)` — every param defaulted to its old constant; `originalsTarget:0` ⇒ vacuously done + note. `routes/brief.ts` reads all six via `getSetting` at request time (`pickAnchors(n, cfg)` gaps/ladder, sparkline query, leader slice, quest helpers); the reply band/ratio stay on `loadDoctrine`. Also updated UI.1's `registry.test.ts` + `settings.test.ts` off the dropped keys (NOT in the plan edit-list — required to keep gates green). See **D128**.
