Namespace-then-delete cleanup (D98c — no read-that-writes here). `--live` = ONE Romanian-idea assist → English (no diacritics) + a `grok` cost row (verified $0.0057 on grok-4.3). Docs: NEW `docs/writer.md`, rewritten `docs/calendar-tab.md` (week board + manual), `docs/composer-tab.md`, `docs/today-tab.md` (§2 Manual post reminder inserted, §1–§18), `docs/people-tab.md`, `docs/settings-tab.md`; the **Authoring 3.0** named entry in `docs/PHASE-HISTORY.md` (D127 — NOT "Phase 10"), a PLAN.md blockquote, a CIRCLES-PLAN.md C5 cross-ref; codemap §8/§9 + header re-stamp. **The Wave-4 D-entries D114–D126 + the A3 header priors + the A3 gotchas were moved verbatim to `STATE-ARCHIVE.md` (§Wave 4) at this task** (the size-discipline archive rule). See **D127**.

### Deviations D1–D5, D9, D10 (pre-seeded, discharged) and D127–D133 (Wave-4 close + the registry lane)

- **D1** (binds AI.2): `app_settings` created once by UI.1; AI.2 reuses it — no second table/migration.

- **D2** (binds UI.2): the 5 doctrine knobs are owned by `niches.doctrine` after N.5; UI.2 must not duplicate them in `app_settings` — read/write through the active niche or drop the keys. **Decision pending at UI.2.**

- **D3** (binds N.4, AI.5): `VOICE_BLOCK_END` renamed by RU.1 to `'## The three variants'`; N.4 substitutes `{{REPLY_PERSONA}}` inside the slice; AI.5 retires slicing — its parity fixture is post-RU/post-N output, and its anti-drift test asserts against defaults containing the niche placeholder.

- **D4** (binds GR.7): no separate `goals` table — extend `me_goals` (baseline fields, `posted_replies`/`originals` metric kinds, `missed` status). One goals system, one digest fact. **Confirm merge shape at GR.7.**

- **D5** (binds RL.4, A3.9, A3.12): after AI.2/AI.6, new LLM calls use `askLLM` + `llmConfigured()`; new prompts register in the registry (`dm`, `article`) so the editor covers them. Refusal-ladder order unchanged.

- **D9** (standing): content.ts task order is fixed: AX.3 → AX.5 → RU.7 → RU.8 → NT.5 → HV.2 → A3.3.

- **D10** (standing): registry knob groups (UI.2–5) run in Wave 5 so they catalog the final constant set, including RU/HV/GR/A3 constants.

- **D127** (A3.15, closes Wave 4): **five plan-vs-repo calls on the docs-sync + smoke, all resolved in the repo's favour.** **(a) The phase title is "Authoring 3.0", NOT the plan's "Phase 10"** — the recent named-phase convention (Harvest 2.0, Guardrails) dropped "Phase N", and "Phase 10" already denotes generated media in `PLAN.md` §"Phased build"; the entry went to `docs/PHASE-HISTORY.md` (D93a) and the PLAN.md note is a top blockquote (the D113a/GR.10 exemplar), never a §"Phased build" edit. **(b) The reconcile is smoked via the EXPORTED `matchManualRows` + a replay of the wrapper's atomic two-sided stamp** — `reconcileManualPosts` is private and its only route (`POST /posts/reconcile`) runs the PAID daily pass, so a $0 smoke can't call the wrapper; the matcher (fully unit-tested at A3.6) + the two-update txn is the faithful $0 realization. **(c) The publisher due-select is replayed read-only** (`status='pending' AND scheduled_for<=now`) — `claimOne` isn't exported and `tickPublisher` needs a token + would call X. **(d) The smoke does NOT re-add the `/writer` shell assertion** (D125b — it lives in `smoke-explorer.ts`); the plan's Task-15 item (e) is discharged there. **(e) The Task-15 codemap bill was mostly already discharged** by A3.1–A3.14's per-task codemap updates (the plan predates that discipline) — A3.15 only added §9 (the smoke) + §8 (the URL-guard manual exemption) + the header re-stamp. **Also:** the whole $0 default run is spend-proof even with `XAI_API_KEY` set (every DM/article assertion is a pre-spend refusal; the 503 is asserted only when `!llmConfigured()`). **Binds nothing forward** except the archive bookkeeping (the Wave-4 D-entries/gotchas/priors moved to `STATE-ARCHIVE.md` §Wave 4).

- **D128** (UI.2, resolves D2/D13/D30c): **the D2 ownership call + four plan-vs-repo divergences, all resolved in the repo's favour.** **(a) D2 = Option B (DROP, not route-through-niche).** The reply band (min/max), week-reply-% and 2–10× multipliers are owned by `niches.doctrine` (N.5, read via `loadDoctrine`); UI.1 had ALSO seeded `x.doctrine.{replyTargetMin,replyTargetMax,weekReplyTargetPct}` in `app_settings`. UI.2 dropped those 3 keys from the registry so each knob has exactly one owner — the D30c-consistent choice ("read/write the 5 through the niche, not the store"; its parenthetical "the anchors/ladderSwitchAt are NOT niche doctrine — those stay `app_settings`" is the tell). The surviving `doctrine` group is the cadence ladder only (`anchors3/anchors4/ladderSwitchAt`). UI.11's Settings tab will link the band to the Niche card (N.7) — a UI concern, not a registry one. **(b) The plan's Task-2 line-refs were stale** — N.5 already moved reply band/ratio to `loadDoctrine` (brief.ts:360-361, 813), so there is no `REPLY_TARGET`/`WEEK_REPLY_TARGET_PCT` module constant at the plan's L42/L43; only anchors/ladder/sparkline/leader were app-level. UI.2's real wiring is brief GAPS (anchors) + ladder pick + `display` + `quests`; the reply-quota/ratio consumers were already niche-wired and are UNTOUCHED. **(c) The plan's Task-2 done-when ("PATCH `x.doctrine.replyTargetMin` → brief quota moves") is IMPOSSIBLE under Option B** — that key no longer exists in the store. The store-owned demonstration instead uses `x.quests.originalsTarget` (deterministic with commitments cleared) + `x.doctrine.anchors3/anchors4` (→ `today.anchors`). **⚠ UI.17's `smoke-settings.ts` MUST pick a store-owned consumer, NOT `replyTargetMin`** (the plan's Measurement § still names it). **(d) Test files not in the plan's edit-list had to change** — dropping the 3 keys broke UI.1's `registry.test.ts` (3 assertions) + `settings.test.ts` (~9); repointed to surviving doctrine keys (`ladderSwitchAt`/`anchors3`), and all PATCH/override tests were kept INSIDE the `doctrine` group so `settings.test.ts`'s `afterEach(resetSettings({group:'doctrine'}))` still cleans up (the settings store is process-global — a stray override leaks between test files). **(e) Target-0 = vacuously-done-with-a-note, NOT literal "omission"** (the plan said "0 ⇒ quest omitted"): omitting a quest would shrink the array and ripple into `allQuestsDone`/`completedMap`/the streak diary; the module's existing loop/launch/targets pattern (target 0 → `done:true` + note) is the faithful, invariant-preserving realization. **Binds forward:** UI.3–UI.5 add their groups the same way (opts-defaulted pure fns, route-only store reads via the bound `getSetting`); **UI.6's extension mirror carries `x.doctrine.anchors3/anchors4/ladderSwitchAt` (`scope:'mirrored'`) into `composerLogic.ts`**; UI.11 owns the Niche-card link for the dropped band keys; UI.17's smoke honors (c).

- **D129** (UI.3, extends D2/D128 a third time): **four plan-vs-repo calls, all resolved in the repo's favour.** **(a) The plan's `x.people.targetBandMinX/MaxX` keys were DROPPED, not added.** The 2–10× target-band multipliers are niche doctrine (`niches.doctrine`, read via `loadDoctrine`) — every `targetBand` caller (`voice.ts`, `routes/followups.ts`, `routes/brief.ts`, `routes/digest.ts`, `routes/playbook.ts`) already passes `{minX: doctrine.targetBandMinX, maxX: doctrine.targetBandMaxX}`. Adding registry keys would be a second silent owner — exactly the D2 drop UI.2 made for the reply band. So `voice.ts` is UNTOUCHED and the `people` group holds only the 3 stage thresholds. The plan's "targetBand mirrored (cosmetic)" How-line predates the D2 resolution and is void. **(b) `computeStage`'s thresholds are read by `people/store.ts::recomputePerson`, not threaded from routes.** `computeStage(events, now, thresholds=STAGE_DEFAULTS)` is a pure defaulted param, but the store read lives in `recomputePerson` (the impure DB function) because it runs from EVERY ingest path (mentions/harvest/engagements/PATCH) — a route-only knob would let those paths advance a stage on the default ladder. This is the ONE sanctioned exception to "only routes/workers read the store" (Decision 6 targets PURE modules; recomputePerson is DB I/O). Only affects future recomputes — the ratchet never demotes. **(c) The digest reads the `followups` group for its neglected windows** (`neglectedTargetDays`/`neglectedAllyDays`) as a second consumer, plus its own new `x.digest.neglectedCap` — no `x.digest.neglected*Days` keys were minted (single owner = the follow-up queue; the weekly list now moves with the daily queue). This makes "digest windows (digest route)" in the plan's Edit line meaningful without duplication. **(d) A route done-when used a store-owned consumer directly.** The followups route test seeds a fresh 16-day-quiet `mutual` and asserts `setSettings({'x.followups.neglectedAllyDays':21})` drops it from the queue, resetting in `finally` (the store is process-global — UI.2 gotcha #3). **Binds forward:** UI.4/UI.5 add their groups the same way; UI.11's Settings tab groups `x.quests.neglectedTargetDays` (daily quest) and `x.followups.neglectedTargetDays` (queue + digest) as two DISTINCT knobs of the same shape — they were minted separately by design (Task 2 vs Task 3), so a user can tune the quest and the queue independently. `x.pinned.outperformRatio` is the first fractional knob (min 1.5, step 0.5) — validation already handled non-integer `number`s, no change needed.

- **D130** (UI.4, extends D128/D129): **six plan-vs-repo calls, all resolved in the repo's favour.** **(a) The plan's Edit line says "registry groups `gates` + `workers`" but its own table mints an `x.radar.*` key** — group ids follow the key's second segment (the `pinned`/`digest` precedent), so UI.4 landed **three** groups: `gates`, `radar`, `workers`, inserted between `digest` and `display` in `GROUP_LABELS` (render order is cosmetic and UI.11 rebuilds the tab anyway). **(b) The winner-re-read knobs are NOT restart-scoped.** The plan's How-line says "startXWorkers reads worker knobs ONCE", but its own table marks only `dailyMetricsHourUtc`/`publisherIntervalSec` as restart. Only those two arm a timer; the re-read floor/cap are per-run query bounds, so `runDailyMetrics` reads them at the START of each run (worker = a sanctioned store consumer, Decision 6) with `RunOptions.winnerRereadMinViews/Cap` as explicit overrides for tests and the manual `POST /x/posts/reconcile`. Net effect: a change lands on the next 03:00 pass with no restart, and the money bound is still evaluated before any billed call. `cap <= 0` returns **before** the candidate query AND its claim write (§7.3 intact). **(c) Env precedence is implemented as a validated registry default.** `envNumberDefault(name, fallback, min, max)` in `registry.ts` ignores (and `console.warn`s) a non-finite or out-of-range env value — otherwise a typo in the unit file would hand the store a default its own validator rejects. A new test asserts **every** registry default validates against its own def, so this can't regress for any future env-backed knob. **(d) The done-when's "12-sample cell" needed a fixture that didn't exist.** `playbook.test.ts`'s shared rows top out at n=2 and `minCellN`'s floor is 5, so the flip is demonstrated on the **timeline funnel** in a NEW last-in-file describe with its own `harvestRuns` id (12 rows, deleted in `afterAll`) — the only playbook dimension whose population a test can own outright without moving another suite's medians (the HV.5 block is the exemplar and its own rows are already gone by then). **(e) Two consumers were added that the plan's Edit list doesn't name, both for single-owner coherence** (the D129c precedent): `routes/brief.ts` reads `x.gates.bestTimeMinN` for the cadence gaps (pure `annotateGaps` gained a trailing defaulted `minN`) so Today and `/metrics/best-times` can't disagree about what "enough data" means; and `loadReplyGuidance`/`loadPostGuidance` read `x.gates.minCellN` so the injected prompt guidance keeps its documented property of never sitting on a thinner bar than the page (their old comment said "the DEFAULT gate" — with the knob, the configured value IS the default). **(f) `x_update_setting` is a WRITE-tier tool, `x_settings` a curated one** → 3/15/5 = 23. **Binds forward:** UI.5 adds `budgets`/`ai`/`mentions` the same way and MUST re-bump the mcp count only if it adds tools (it doesn't); **UI.6's extension mirror carries `x.gates.bestTimeMinN` (`scope:'mirrored'`) into `composerLogic.ts`, whose baked `BEST_TIME_MIN_N = 3` becomes the fallback**; UI.11 renders the three new groups (the two restart knobs need the "takes effect on restart" hint — `appliesOn` is already on the wire).

- **D131** (UI.5, extends D128/D129/D130): **nine plan-vs-repo calls, all resolved in the repo's favour, plus the ownership question the plan didn't ask.** **(a) The plan's `x.ai.*` knobs looked like a second owner of the AI.2 `ai` blob — they are not, and that is the load-bearing finding.** `askLLM`'s precedence is `request body > the DB 'ai' settings > call-site house defaults`, and the `ai` blob's own comment says `null = each call site's own house default wins`. The registry knobs ARE that last tier, so they tune exactly what the blob defers to. No key was dropped (unlike D2/D128/D129): the two layers are a global override and a per-surface default, which is a real distinction a user can want. The descriptions say so. **(b) The plan's `x.ai.replyMaxOutputTokens` default of 350 is STALE — it is 520.** RU.1 raised the cap for the third variant and left the reason in the code ("verified live under the old 350 cap for two variants"). Registering 350 would have silently shrunk every reply draft and truncated variant three — a paid call that then 502s on a parse error. The FLOOR is 300, not the plan's 100, for the same reason: the measured three-variant need is ~225 output tokens, so 300 is the lowest value that cannot buy a truncated draft (Decision 5 — the registry bound IS the money guard, and this is the one number the plan's author didn't have). **(c) `x.mentions.pullMax` must be a SEPARATE opt, never `maxResults`.** CA.1 made `opts.maxResults !== undefined` the caller-intent flag that switches `maxTotal` from the 800 hard cap down to one page; passing the configured page size as `maxResults` would re-cap every incremental pull at one page — literally the bug CA.1 fixed (50 mentions/day silently dropped in prod). `pullMentions` now takes `{maxResults?, pullMax?}` and `pageMax = maxResults ?? pullMax ?? DEFAULT_PULL_MAX`. **Generalized in codemap §7:** a knob whose call site already carries a caller-intent flag gets its own opt. **(d) `workers/dailyMetrics.ts` was not in the plan's edit list but reads `pullMax` too** (D129c/D130e single-owner precedent — the worker is a sanctioned store consumer, and "mentions per page" must not mean two things). **(e) `x.ai.batchReplyCap` is `server`, not the plan's `mirrored`.** Its note says "RADAR_DRAFT_CAP stays ≤ this" — **no such constant exists anywhere**, `Radar.tsx` sends every undrafted sighting with no client cap (the server 400 `too_many_tweets` is the only bound), and UI.6's edit list doesn't include Radar.tsx. A mirrored key with no reader is dead weight in the blob. **(f) Three orphaned constants were DELETED, not kept as fallbacks.** Once the registry owns a number, a same-named module constant next to it is exactly the second-silent-owner smell this wave has been removing: `MAX_OUTPUT_TOKENS`/`DEFAULT_TEMPERATURE`/`DEFAULT_REASONING` are gone from `replies.ts`, `MAX_OUTPUT_TOKENS` from `drafter.ts`, `DIGEST_MAX_OUTPUT_TOKENS` from `digest.ts` (their sizing rationale moved into `replyLlmDefaults()`'s doc comment and the registry descriptions). `MAX_BATCH_TWEETS` and `MAX_REFRESHES_PER_DAY` SURVIVE — they are the defaults of pure params (`parseBatchTweets(value, maxTweets)`, `takeRefreshSlot(state, now, max)`), the D130a shape. **(g) `0` means zero.** `images.ts`'s old `imageBudgetUsd()` treated a 0 env value as invalid and fell back to $0.50; as a setting, `imageDailyUsd: 0` must genuinely disable generation (the plan's own done-when). Range starts at 0 for the image budget but at 0.01 for the soft X budget, whose floor keeps the watchdog meaningful. **(h) Two test files not in the plan's edit list had to change.** `images.test.ts`'s 429 test set `process.env.XAI_IMAGE_DAILY_BUDGET_USD` at runtime, which is now a no-op (env is read once at registry import) — repointed onto `setSettings`, which also makes it deterministic whatever the dev's `.env` holds. `registry.test.ts`'s group id/label lists are exact and had to grow (the D128d situation). **(i) `scripts/smoke-studio.ts` had the same dead env write** and was repaired in the same commit: it now pins the budget through the store, and because it runs against the REAL DB it snapshot-restores the operator's own override from the success path AND from `fail()` (via a module-level `restoreSettings` hook — `fail()` calls `process.exit`, so no `finally` would run). Verified end-to-end: smoke passes and `app_settings` is empty afterwards. **Binds forward:** **UI.6 carries `x.mentions.panelRefreshCap` into `Conversations.tsx` (baked `REFRESH_LIMIT = 4` becomes the fallback)** — 5 mirrored keys total, see next-up; UI.7's band knobs are the next `mirrored` pair; UI.11 renders the `budgets`/`ai`/`mentions` groups and should surface the two-tier AI story (per-surface default vs the global `ai` blob) so the Settings tab doesn't read as two competing controls; **UI.17's `smoke-settings.ts` should copy (i)'s snapshot-restore shape**, not a bare reset.

- **D132** (UI.6, first Wave-5 extension task): **five plan-vs-repo calls, all resolved in the repo's favour, plus the layering call the plan didn't make.** **(a) `DoNext.tsx` was DROPPED from the edit list.** Task 6 says "Conversations.tsx, DoNext.tsx — panel refresh cap, strip cap, snooze hours from the hook", but `x.display.doNextCap`/`doNextSnoozeH` **do not exist** — the plan's OWN Task 12 (UI.12) mints them. A hook read of a key that isn't in the registry is dead code that silently keeps the baked constant, so DoNext's `STRIP_CAP`/`SNOOZE_HOURS` stay baked until UI.12 mints the keys AND wires them in the same task. **(b) `Calendar.tsx` + `calendarLogic.ts` were ADDED to the edit list** (A3.14 post-dates the plan): they are the second consumer of `pickAnchors`/`findScheduleGaps`/`bestTimeCellScore`/`slotHint`/`suggestBestSlotDate`, so leaving them baked would have put the board's ghost anchors and the Composer's "Best time" on two different ladders **on adjacent tabs**. `buildWeekBoard` gained the same trailing `cfg`. The D130e single-owner precedent. **(c) The plan's `suggestBestSlotDate(cells, taken, now, opts = LADDER_DEFAULTS)` signature is fiction** — the real one is `(now, scheduledLocal, cells, horizonDays, rand, audience?)` after A3.4, so the config went on as a **7th trailing defaulted param** (the A3.4 shape), which is what keeps every existing call site and the whole `composerLogic.test.ts` byte-valid. **(d) `serverSettings.ts` owns the baked fallbacks; `composerLogic` derives from it, not the other way round.** `shared/` may not import `sidepanel/`, and two copies of `[9,13,18]` inside the extension is exactly the second-silent-owner smell D131f removed on the server — so `ANCHORS_3`/`ANCHORS_4`/`BEST_TIME_MIN_N`/`CADENCE_DEFAULTS` are now projections of `SERVER_DEFAULTS`. The module has **zero imports** so Vite can inline it into the content-script IIFE at UI.7 (§7.26). **(e) The generic `readServerSetting(blob, key, fallback)` the plan names was not built** — `readServerConfig(raw): ServerConfig` is what every consumer actually wants, and it makes "a mirrored key with no baked fallback" impossible to read by accident. The fallback is applied **per key** (shape guards only; the registry already validated range at write time), so one corrupt value can't drop the rest to baked. **(f) The panel-mount sync lives in `App.tsx`, not in the hook** — three mounted consumers must not mean three GETs — and every `settingsClient` write fires one too, so future gear popovers inherit the invalidation for free. **Binds forward:** **UI.7** adds the `x.band.*` keys as `ServerConfig` fields + `SERVER_DEFAULTS` entries and is the FIRST content-script consumer (init read + `chrome.storage.onChanged` on `settings:server`, `local` area — the blob is deliberately in `local`, not `session`, because content scripts can't see session and a cold panel should open on the last known config); it must also update the exact-list assertion in `registry.test.ts`. UI.11's Settings tab writes through `settingsClient`, so its saves already re-sync. UI.12 owns DoNext per (a). UI.13 owns the Composer/Calendar polish and must keep passing `server` into BOTH.

- **D133** (UI.7, extends D128–D132): **three plan-vs-repo calls and two the plan didn't make, all resolved in the repo's favour.** **(a) The plan's registry table lists 10 band keys; 12 shipped.** Its own How-line hoists FOUR inline literals (`tooSmallAgeMin:20, tooSmallViews:300, tooSmallVpm:15, watchReplyCeiling:25`) into `BandThresholds` but then registers only two of them — leaving `tooSmallAgeMin`/`tooSmallVpm` as numbers a rebuild alone could change, sitting inside a three-condition rule whose third part IS tunable. **Half a rule is worse than an unexposed one**: the part a user can't reach reads as a bug, and UI.11 would render the gap. So the `band` group is exactly `keyof BandThresholds` (a registry test asserts that set equality, and that each default `=== BAND[key]`), and every key is `mirrored` — a server-only band knob would be the one thing this feature exists to prevent (badge and gate disagreeing). **(b) `serverSettings.ts` is no longer literally import-free, and the "ZERO imports" rule was always a proxy.** It now value-imports `BAND` from `../replyBand.ts`. The real constraint (§7.26, D132d) is "nothing that can't be inlined into the content-script IIFE" — no React, no sidepanel module, nothing with its own dependencies — and `replyBand.ts` is the one module content.ts already inlines. The alternative was re-typing twelve numbers as a second owner, exactly the D131f/D132d smell. `dist/content.js` was checked after the build: 136 kB, **zero `import` statements**; that grep is the standing check, not the import count. **(c) `src/x/playbook.ts` isn't in the plan's edit list but holds two `classifyBand` call sites, and they were split deliberately.** `buildTimelineFunnel`/`deriveTimelineBand` (the HV.5 opportunity funnel) TAKE the configured thresholds: the band there is derived at read time and never stored (§7.12), and the cell measures how well you capture what the badge highlights — on baked defaults it would describe a badge you never saw. `scoreReplyOutcome`'s fallback deliberately does NOT: most rows in that crosstab carry the band **stamped at capture time**, and that table is the evidence a recalibration is judged on (§7.19), so relabelling the unstamped minority with today's numbers would mix two vintages inside one table and make the evidence move whenever the knob does. Both reasons are in the code as comments. **(d) The route half of the done-when can't be tested in the loosening direction.** "PATCH → the post is no longer gated" means the handler proceeds to a REAL Grok call (`/x/replies/generate` mounts whenever a key is configured, and `.env` has one). The two shapes that work: tighten `bigViews` until a default-`hot` context 422s, and pick a context that 422s under BOTH settings so the reported `band` field flipping `skip`→`null` is what proves the store was read. Generalized into codemap §9. **(e) The hoist surfaced a property worth knowing: at the shipped defaults the dead-zone early return never changes a verdict.** It fires only when `views < tooSmallViews` (== `bigViews`) AND `ageMin > tooSmallAgeMin` (> `freshMin`), and a post failing both can neither clear the view floor nor reach a velocity path — so it is a short-circuit, not a decision. That is *why* `tooSmallViews` shadows `bigViews` at 300 and why they are separate knobs: raising the "worth a reply" floor cannot widen the dead zone. A test pins the property (classify with the branch switched off ⇒ identical verdicts across a spread of signals). **Binds forward:** UI.11 renders the `band` group and should sit it next to the Niche card's reply band (different owners, adjacent meaning — D128a); UI.15's Playbook polish inherits the funnel-vs-crosstab split, so don't "unify" the two classifiers; UI.16 owns `content.ts` next and must keep `initBandThresholds()` in `start()`; **UI.17's `smoke-settings.ts` gets a free $0 consumer demonstration here** — a band PATCH moves `GET /x/playbook`'s `timelineFunnel` with no spend at all, which is cheaper than the brief-quota route D128c pointed it at.

### Gotchas archived at UI.11

- **`bun run lint` was 114–119 pre-existing errors at baseline** — mostly the `Stratus Design System/` reference folder. **UI.8 (D11) added that folder to biome ignore → whole-repo now 7 errors**, all pre-existing and outside any changed file (2 a11y `Replies.tsx`, 5 format on generated `migrations/meta/*.json`). Per-task gate stays "the changed files lint clean" (`bunx biome check <files>`), not "whole-repo green"; don't chase the remaining 7 unless your task owns those files.

- **RL.8: `cellOf` counts `n` only for MEASURED rows** (those with a `metrics_snapshots` row); `posted` counts every attributed reply. The smoke seeds a snapshot so both move by +1 — a future assertion that publishes a reply without a snapshot must assert `canned.posted`, not `canned.n`, or it will read as a silent failure.

- **NT.2: a test handle longer than 15 chars is silently skipped and the whole test goes vacuous.** `normalizePersonHandle` enforces `/^[A-Za-z0-9_]{1,15}$/`, so `nt2_mutual_person` (17) was dropped by `recordEngagements` while every assertion around it still "passed" (the person row had been inserted by hand). Cost a debug round-trip. Two rules for any people-layer test: keep seeded handles **≤15 chars**, and assert `{processed, skipped}` on the ingest result so a skip can never hide behind downstream assertions.

- **NT.2: seeding `posts_published` in the shared in-memory DB is safe only with an out-of-window date and no metrics row.** The engagements suite needs own posts for target resolution; it uses a **January `postedAt`** and writes no `metrics_snapshots`, which keeps the rows out of every windowed or measured aggregation other suites assert over (playbook medians, reup 14–60d, brief/digest/quest windows, pinned watch). Files run sequentially so the only real hazard is a stray — cleanup runs inline *and* in `afterAll`. Reuse this shape rather than inventing a new seed.

- **NT.3 test-handle rule (again):** the route suite uses `nt3_route_eng` (13 chars). The NT.2 gotcha applies unchanged — any people-layer test handle over 15 chars is silently skipped by `normalizePersonHandle`, and asserting `{processed, skipped}` is what keeps that from hiding.

- **NT.7: a real-DB smoke that seeds `posts_published` must write the row `retired: true`.** `scripts/smoke-notifications.ts` needs an own post for prefix target resolution; a non-retired one left behind by an aborted run is a candidate for the daily 03:00 UTC snapshot pass, i.e. a **real billed X read** for a tweet id that doesn't exist (invariant #7). `retired: true` takes it out of the candidate set permanently and costs the smoke nothing (it never reads metrics). Reuse this whenever a smoke seeds own posts against the real DB — `smoke-followups.ts` predates the rule and gets away with it only because its rows are deleted synchronously.

- **UI.2 (Wave-5 opener) — three facts the settings moat now binds:** **(1)** the reply band / week-reply-% / 2–10× multipliers are **NOT settings-store keys** — they live on `niches.doctrine`, read via `loadDoctrine()` (voice/brief/followups/playbook/digest). A future task tuning them edits the niche (`PATCH /x/niches/:slug`), never `app_settings`; the registry's `doctrine` group is now ONLY `anchors3/anchors4/ladderSwitchAt`. **(2)** `routes/brief.ts` reads six store keys via the bound `getSetting` (from `../settings/registry.ts`, never the store) at REQUEST time — sync Map-cached, invalidated on write — so a `PATCH /x/settings` moves the next `GET /x/brief` with no restart (the `appliesOn:'immediate'` contract; worker-cadence knobs will be `'restart'`). **(3)** the settings store is **process-global across test files** (one in-memory `app_settings` + one module cache). Any test that `setSettings(...)` MUST reset it in `afterEach`/`afterAll` (`resetSettings({group})` or `{keys}`) or the override leaks into a later suite — `settings.test.ts` + `brief.test.ts` both do; keep new store PATCH assertions inside one registry group so a group-reset cleans them.

- **UI.4 — four facts the next settings task needs:** **(1) A test that PATCHes a knob whose consumer is a GLOBAL read-that-writes must bound the blast radius.** `GET /radar/drafts` expires **every** stale `ready` row, not just the caller's, so the TTL test seeds its own 10h-old row and only ever shortens the window to 6h (rows other suites insert default `draftedAt` to now and are untouched). Same shape as GR.10's snapshot-restore rule, one notch cheaper. **(2) The `?param=` vs knob contract is now precedent:** a query param overrides the configured baseline **per read**, in both directions (`?minN=20` re-tightens a store-loosened gate). Any future "the knob is the default" task should assert both directions like `playbook.test.ts` does. **(3) `appliesOn` is a real behavioral split, not UI copy** — a knob is `'restart'` **only** if reading it later can't take effect (it armed a timer). Everything else reads per request/run, even inside a worker. Getting this backwards makes a knob look broken (silently ignored until a deploy). **(4) `startXWorkers` now touches the DB at boot.** It reads two settings before starting any timer; that's safe because `src/db/client.ts` migrates at import time, long before `app.ts` calls it — but a future refactor that makes worker startup independent of the db client import would break it.

- **UI.5 — four facts the next settings/money task needs:** **(1) An env-backed registry default is read ONCE, at registry import.** Every `process.env.X = …` written at runtime by a test or a script to steer such a knob is now a silent no-op that still *looks* like it works (both `images.test.ts` and `smoke-studio.ts` had one, and both passed only because `.env` happened to hold the same number). Steer the knob through `setSettings`, and audit for a runtime env write whenever you move a constant into the registry. **(2) A smoke that PATCHes a setting must snapshot-restore, not reset** — `resetSettings` DELETES the row, which silently wipes an override the operator set on purpose. `resolveSetting(key)` first, then restore by `isDefault` (smoke-studio is the exemplar), and hang it off `fail()` too, since `fail()` exits. **(3) The AI layer has TWO legitimate tiers and they are not duplicates:** `x.ai.*` (per-surface house default) and the `ai` blob from Settings → AI (global override, `null` = defer). If a future task "unifies" them it will silently take the per-surface knobs out of the merge — the `ai` blob wins over them by design. **(4) `getDailyBudgetUsd` is now called on every cost event** (it resolves the getter), i.e. one sync settings read per billed call — Map-cached and invalidated on write, so it stays sub-microsecond; a future change making the store read expensive would put that cost on the paying path.

- **UI.6 — five facts the next extension/settings task needs:** **(1) Adding a `scope:'mirrored'` key is a 2-file move**, and `src/x/settings/registry.test.ts` now asserts the mirrored key list EXACTLY (5 keys) so you can't forget the second file: the registry def, then a `ServerConfig` field + `SERVER_DEFAULTS` fallback in `extension/src/shared/serverSettings.ts`. Without the second, the key ships in the blob and is never read — no type error, no test failure, just a knob that does nothing on the client. **(2) A cap that arrives asynchronously must be applied to a SPENT counter, not snapshotted as REMAINING.** `Conversations.tsx` used to `useState(REFRESH_LIMIT - used)`; with the cap arriving from the blob after mount (and changeable mid-session by a save) that snapshot is stale the moment it's taken — it now stores `refreshesUsed` and derives `refreshesLeft`. Any future mirrored knob feeding an initial `useState` has the same trap. **(3) `bun run lint` is now whole-repo clean (0 errors)** — the two `Replies.tsx` a11y errors that STATE has described as "pre-existing, don't chase" since UI.8 are gone. A new lint error is now yours. **(4) The `.claude/skills/stratus/scripts/` files are linted by biome but NOT typechecked** (root `tsconfig.json` includes only `src/**`, `scripts/**`, `drizzle.config.ts` — the repo-root `scripts/`, not the skill's). A type error in `md_to_schedule.ts` is invisible to every gate; it is verified by running it. **(5) Bun auto-loads `.env` into `process.env`**, which is why the skill script resolves `STRATUS_API_TOKEN ?? API_TOKEN` (the repo's `.env` has only `API_TOKEN`, and api.sh does the same mapping) — and why `env -u API_TOKEN bun run …` does NOT unset it. Don't try to prove a no-token fallback path that way.

- **UI.7 — four facts the next settings/extension task needs:** **(1) A knob whose LOOSENING direction spends can only be route-tested on the refusal side.** `/x/replies/generate` mounts whenever any LLM key is configured (`.env` has one), so "PATCH → the post gets through" runs a real paid Grok call inside `bun run test`. Assert a tightening that 422s, and prove the store was read by picking a context that refuses under BOTH settings so the reported `band` changes (`skip`→`null`) — the permissive direction belongs in the pure suite. Any future gate knob (a future spend guard, a future refusal threshold) has the same shape. **(2) "`serverSettings.ts` has zero imports" is retired as a literal rule** — it now imports `BAND` from `../replyBand.ts`. What actually matters is that nothing reaching that module drags something un-inlinable into the content-script IIFE. The check is `grep -cE '^\s*import[ {]' extension/dist/content.js` after `bun run build` (must be 0), not the import count in source. **(3) The extension's `chrome.storage.onChanged` listeners are per-init and never removed** — `content.ts` now registers a fourth (`initBandThresholds`, beside passiveCapture/passiveHarvest/typeFromClipboard). They all filter `area !== 'local'` first; a future init that forgets that filter would react to `sync`/`session` writes. **(4) The settings store is read once per gated request now, on a hot path.** `bandThresholdsFromSettings()` is 12 `getSetting` calls per `/x/replies/generate` and per `GET /x/playbook` — all Map-cached and sync, so sub-microsecond, but the same UI.5 caveat applies: anything that makes a store read expensive lands on the reply path.

## Archived at UI.17 (2026-07-25) — the Wave-5 polish lane (UI.11–UI.16), closing the masterplan

> Moved verbatim by line number from STATE.md at the masterplan's final task. Wave 5 is closed;
> nothing below binds an open task. Grep by task id (UI.11 … UI.16) or by filename.
### header priors (UI.15, UI.14, UI.16, UI.13, UI.12, UI.11) — moved verbatim from STATE.md lines 17–22

- **prior (UI.15 — high, deps UI.10✓+UI.4✓+UI.5✓, $0, server registry + extension + docs, no schema/migration/route/MCP change, 17 files [0 new]; suite 1563 unchanged):** the last four pre-masterplan tabs joined the design system and the Playbook's gate became editable in place. **Playbook is the showcase:** fifteen `.brief-section`s → `Section`, eight bare-`.muted` empties → `EmptyState` with a second coach line, and every gated cell now reads **`insufficient data (n=7/20)`** — the gate it fell short of, not just its own n, rendered by a NEW `ResultCell` component (the string-returning `cellSummary` is gone) in a NEW `.pb-gated` span. That second number closed a four-phase-old latent bug: `cellSummary(c, _minN)` had ignored its gate param since C4. The header ⚙ tunes `x.gates.minCellN` (**already registered and `server`-scoped — no registry edit, the plan was right**) and the page **re-reads itself once** `PATCH_DEBOUNCE_MS + 300` after the value settles, because every `sufficient` flag is computed server-side; a `reloadedForGate` ref bounds it to one re-read per distinct value. Registry **60→61 knobs**, mirrored **26→27**: `x.display.repliesListLimit` (100, 20–500) behind a ⚙ on Replies' *History* `Section`, with `loadHistory`'s dep array widened. `chips.ts` gained `replyDraftChip` (a sixth vocabulary, not a sixth family), retiring Replies' borrow of the scheduled-post `.badge-*` ramp; `.reply-variant` was **aliased** into `.chip`. Harvest's mode + date-range + pace are one `SubTabs`, which gained the `disabled` prop its CSS anticipated at UI.10; `.seg-group`/`.seg-row` deleted. Studio's `KitEditor` is three `Section`s; its background row is `SubTabs`. Docs: `playbook-tab.md` (the "no button in this tab to loosen it" paragraph was FALSE), `replies-tab.md`, `harvest-tab.md`, `s3-studio.md`, `settings-tab.md` (gear list 9→11). **Gates:** 1563/0, both typechecks, whole-repo lint, extension build, `content.js` imports 0, `smoke-playbook` + `smoke-passive-harvest` + `smoke-studio` + `smoke-reply-lists` all green. **Browser-verify owed** — see next-up. See **D139**.
- **prior (UI.14 — high, deps UI.10✓+UI.3✓, $0, server registry + extension + docs, no schema/migration/route/MCP change, 13 files [1 new]; suite 1562→1563 [+1]):** the four CRM/library tabs joined the design system and the panel got ONE chip family. `styles.css` carries a documented `.chip` block — one shape, five tones (`ok`/`accent`/`warn`/`strong`/`muted`) — that **absorbed** `.stage-chip`, `.people-angle-chip` and `.dm-status`, with NEW `sidepanel/chips.ts` mapping each vocabulary (CRM stage, idea lifecycle, following-ledger status, DM status, swipe-file author state) onto a tone; `.badge-*`, `.radar-band-*` and the GR.8 pacing chip stay OUT on purpose (different alphabets — the GR.8 comment already said why). **The family is an ALIAS, not the plan's rename**, because `.stage-chip` is rendered by five files and three are UI.15's (D138b). Registry **57→60 knobs**, mirrored **23→26**: `x.display.dossierListLen` (5, 3–25), `channelPostsShown` (8, 3–30), `voiceListLimit` (100, 20–500), each wired to a gear beside the list it caps — People's **dossier** header (not the plan's roster header: the knob caps three lists, two of them conditional; the timeline stays uncapped and the `note` says so), the Channels room's *My posts* section, and Voice's new *Saved tweets* section. Every `.brief-section` on the four tabs is `Section` + `EmptyState` (`people-head` and `people-quicklog` stay bare cards — D135e); the four hand-rolled pill rows are `SubTabs`; `EVENT_ICONS` is text glyphs (`❑ ❐ ↗ ↘ ⇄ ◔ ≋ ✎ ✉ ♥ ⟳ ✚`); `StagePicker` is the six-rung ladder as `aria-pressed` chip buttons on its own row. **`.ui-subtabs` gained `flex-wrap`** — the seven-tab stage filter would have run off the panel, and the rule the migration deleted (`.people-stage-filter`) was that wrap. Three dead rules removed. `docs/people-tab.md` + `channels-tab.md` + `voice-tab.md` + `ideas-tab.md` + `settings-tab.md` (gear list 6→9) updated. **Gates:** 1563/0, both typechecks, whole-repo lint, extension build, `content.js` imports 0, `smoke-people.ts` + `smoke-channels.ts` green. **Browser-verify owed** — see next-up. See **D138**.
- **prior (UI.16 — high, dep UI.8✓, $0, extension + the two public pages + docs, no server/registry/schema/migration/route/MCP change, 6 files [0 new]; suite 1562 unchanged):** the last three hand-styled surfaces joined the design system. `content.ts::injectStyles` now opens with **`OVERLAY_TOKENS`**, one `:root{}` block of 21 `--stratus-*` values (`<tone>`/`-line`/`-fill` for muted/blue/hot/warm/danger/pillar + `-muted-hairline` + `--stratus-font`), and every rule below it is `var()`-only — ~100 copy-pasted literals across the band border, person chips, notification chips + sync button, channel chips, context panel and variant chips collapsed to one place, with alphas aligned (fills `.12`, colored outlines `.5`, muted `.4`, dividers `.3`; every delta ≤0.05). The colors are unchanged and stay **theme-neutral on purpose** — an overlay wears X's theme, so `--strat-*` must never appear there (D137c). `public/explorer.html` + `public/writer.html` moved onto the DS palette written ONCE per file with `light-dark()` (`color-scheme: light dark` = the `prefers-color-scheme` arm) plus short aliases their existing rules resolve through, a shared **◐ system / ☾ dark / ☀ light** header button persisting `explorer:theme`, and a `<head>` boot IIFE so a pinned theme never flashes; the writer additionally got its pillar chip on `--strat-pillar` and a primary-button hover. `docs/s1-data-explorer.md` (the "dark GitHub-style theme" line was now false), `docs/writer.md` and `docs/s6-augmented-ui.md` updated. **Gates:** 1562/0, both typechecks, lint, extension build, `content.js` imports still 0, `smoke-explorer.ts` green, plus a dangling/dead-`var()` audit (21/21 + 30/30 + 30/30). **Browser-verify owed** — see next-up. See **D137**.
- **prior (UI.13 — high, deps UI.10✓+UI.6✓, $0, extension + docs, no server/registry/schema/migration/route/MCP change, 9 files [0 new]; suite 1560→1562 [+2]):** the cadence ladder got its affordance on both surfaces that render it. ONE gear — `composerLogic.ts::CADENCE_SETTING_KEYS` = `x.doctrine.anchors3`/`anchors4`/`ladderSwitchAt` + `x.gates.bestTimeMinN`, all already mirrored — hangs off the Composer's NEW `Section title="Schedule"` (publish-mode + datetime row + hints + cost line, in that order) and the Calendar's NEW `Section title="This week"`, each with its own ownership `note`. **The first `numberArray` gear found two primitive bugs, both fixed in `ui/`:** `GearPopover` blurs the focused descendant before closing (unmount eats the `blur` that commits the row) and `NumberArrayInput` swallows Enter (the gear is inside the Composer's `<form>`). Other polish: `thinCellsForWeekday` + a dimmed "Wed so far: 17:xx (n=2)" middle state, every "need ≥3" string now interpolating `server.bestTimeMinN` (configurable since UI.6, the copy never caught up), `counterClass` ramping neutral → `.counter.near` → `.counter.over`, the drafter head (`Draft with AI`, the `§8.1`/`§8.2` leak gone) and the drafts tray onto `Section` with the count in the title, a week-empty `EmptyState` + per-column "Anchors have passed.", and the `.drafter h3` rule + two double-spacing margins deleted. `docs/composer-tab.md` + `docs/calendar-tab.md` + `docs/settings-tab.md`'s gear list updated. **Browser-verify owed** — see next-up. See **D136**.
- **prior (UI.12 — high, deps UI.10✓+UI.2✓+UI.6✓, $0, server registry + extension + docs, no schema/migration/route/MCP change, 20 files [2 new]; suite 1549→1560 [+11]):** the moat got affordances where the features are. Registry **53→57 knobs** (4 new `x.display.*` Today caps) and **mirrored 17→23** — the four plus two SCOPE FLIPS (`x.followups.neglectedTargetDays`, `x.ai.batchReplyCap`) chosen over minting panel twins, which is D135b's general rule. `DoNext`'s `STRIP_CAP`/`SNOOZE_HOURS`, `Fans`' `AMBER_TOP_N`, `Targets`' `NEGLECT_DAYS` and `Radar`'s `RADAR_DRAFT_CAP` are all gone, read from `useServerSettings()`; `radarBatchSize(cfg)` clamps the display cap to the batch cap the server enforces. UI.11's editing discipline was **extracted** into NEW `sidepanel/settingsEditor.ts::useSettingsEditor` (TuningPanel consumes it too — behaviour-identical) with NEW `sidepanel/SettingsGear.tsx` and two new pure helpers in `settingsClient.ts` (`entriesForKeys`, `applyOptimisticValue`, both tested). **Five inline ⚙** (quests / DoNext / Radar / Targets / Fans), each with an ownership `note`; `GearPopover` gained `note` + `errors`. Section/EmptyState across Today + DoNext/Fans/Radar/Targets/Digest/Conversations (LaunchRoom + ManualPostCard stay alert cards); `.radar-head`/`.radar-actions` deleted. `docs/today-tab.md` + `docs/settings-tab.md` updated. **Browser-verify owed** — see next-up. NEW `TuningPanel` renders all 14 groups / 53 knobs from `GET /x/settings` (never the server registry — §5) with a sticky search, per-group **Reset group**, per-knob reset dots and the `restart` chip UI.10 already shipped. A `GROUP_NOTE` map states the ownership the D2/D128/D129 drops created (doctrine/people/band → "that number is niche-owned, General → Niche") and the two-tier AI story (`x.ai.*` = per-surface default, the `ai` blob = global override), with the AI subtab linking straight into it.
